### [1] [Mind the Rift: Cross-Scale Coupling Mismatch for AI-Generated Video Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00742)
*Siyu Li,Jin Yang,Weiheng Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RIFT框架，基于"跨尺度耦合失配"这一新取证信号检测AI生成视频。通过宏流（微分几何与持久同调分析轨迹）、微流（隐写滤波与时序建模）以及耦合散度模块的三角设计，在120K和68K视频基准上分别达到99.33%和99.72%的F1分数，并展现出97.87%的未见生成器检测率和编码器无关性（替换不同规模/家族编码器下性能几乎无退化）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI视频生成器达到电影级真实感，现有深度学习方法依赖特定生成器伪影，泛化能力弱。传统取证特征未能利用自然视频中宏层时间动力学与微层残差模式由统一成像物理流水线所决定的内在耦合关系，而AI生成器训练目标并不显式保持该联合分布。检测的关键挑战在于需要独立提取两个尺度的信息同时量化其跨尺度关系。

Method: 提出三组件正交取证框架RIFT：(1) 宏流通过微分几何与持久同调在学习到的流形轨迹上构建期望时间演化的动态基线；(2) 微流作为敏感取证探针，使用隐写滤波与时序建模提取像素级残差；(3) 耦合散度模块量化两流之间的条件依赖。Gram-Schmidt正交化保证测量的信息论有效性，确保两流提取的信息相互独立。

Result: 在VidProM（120K视频，7个生成器）上达到99.33% F1，在GenVidBench（68K视频，4个生成器）上达到99.72% F1；留一法评估中未见生成器检测率达97.87%。编码器维度上高度稳健：从ViT-S/14(22M)扩展到ViT-L/14(300M)性能变化小于0.1%，切换到DINOv1家族仅下降0.73个百分点。

Conclusion: RIFT通过揭示并量化AI视频生成中跨尺度耦合的违反，构建了首个利用物理成像先验耦合关系的正交取证框架。实验结果证明了其高精度、强泛化能力和编码器无关性，为AI生成视频检测提供了具有理论依据且可扩展的新型范式。

Abstract: As AI video generators achieve cinematic realism, reliable detection becomes essential for safeguarding digital trust. We identify cross-scale coupling mismatch as a new forensic signal, where scale refers to the level of abstraction (semantic dynamics vs. pixel-level residuals): in natural videos, macro-level temporal dynamics and micro-level residual patterns are intrinsically coupled by the unified imaging physics pipeline, whereas AI generators, whose training objectives do not explicitly preserve this joint distribution, systematically violate this coupling. Detecting such mismatch is challenging because it requires independently extracting information at both scales while simultaneously quantifying their cross-scale relationship. We propose RIFT (Representation Inconsistency Forensics on Trajectories), an orthogonal forensic framework that addresses this through three interlocking components: a macro stream that builds a dynamic baseline of expected temporal evolution via differential geometry and persistent homology on learned manifold trajectories, a micro stream that acts as a sensitive forensic probe via steganalytic filtering and temporal modeling, and a coupling divergence module that measures the conditional dependency between the two streams. Gram-Schmidt orthogonality guarantees the information-theoretic validity of this measurement. Experiments on two benchmarks (VidProM, 120K videos, 7 generators; GenVidBench, 68K videos, 4 generators) demonstrate that RIFT achieves 99.33% and 99.72% F1-score respectively, with 97.87% unseen-generator detection rate in leave-one-out evaluation, while exhibiting encoder agnosticism: scaling from ViT-S/14 (22M) to ViT-L/14 (300M) changes F1 by less than 0.1%, and switching to a different encoder family (DINOv1) reduces F1 by only 0.73 pp. Code is available at https://github.com/Litsay/RIFT

</details>


### [2] [TimeSteer: Inference-Time Speech Scheduling in Joint Audio-Visual Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01277)
*Chao Zhou,Yiling Chen,Qi Chu,Tao Gong,Nenghai Yu,Tianyi We*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TimeSteer，一个无需训练的推理时语音调度框架，利用预训练联合音视频扩散模型的去噪特性，将耦合语音与视觉发音的内容定位并重新映射到用户指定的时间区间内，无需微调骨干模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管预训练的联合音视频扩散模型能灵活控制"生成什么"（what），但无法显式控制语音在何时出现（when）。现有的模型在时间层面缺乏对语音发声区间的细粒度控制能力，亟需一种无需重新训练即可实现语音时间调度的方法。

Method: TimeSteer框架包含两个核心步骤：(1) Source Span Localization——利用去噪过程中时间敏感的文本到音频交叉注意力头来定位每个发声内容在潜在时间线上的源区间；(2) Region-Aware Latent Remapping——利用预测的干净潜在表示已将语音与视觉发音耦合组织的特性，将源区间中的音视频潜在内容转移到指定的目标区间。同时提出SpeechShift基准用于评估区间级语音调度。

Result: 在两个代表性骨干模型上的实验表明，TimeSteer相较于无训练基线在区间可控性上显著提升，同时保持了具有竞争力的整体生成质量。并发布了SpeechShift，首个用于联合音视频生成的区间级语音调度基准。

Conclusion: TimeSteer证明了无需微调即可实现精确的推理时语音时间调度，方法是利用去噪过程中暴露的时间敏感注意力机制和预测潜在表示中耦合的音视频结构，为音视频联合生成提供了实用的细粒度时间控制手段。

Abstract: Although pretrained joint audio-visual diffusion models offer rich control over \emph{what} to generate, they provide no explicit control over \emph{when} an utterance should occur. To address this, we study \emph{inference-time speech scheduling}, a novel task that places coupled speech and visual articulation within user-specified begin--end intervals without finetuning the backbone model. We uncover two intrinsic properties of the denoising process that enable this task. First, a timing-sensitive text-to-audio cross-attention head exposes each utterance's model-implied source span along the latent timeline. Second, the predicted clean latent already organizes coupled speech and visual articulation, allowing their temporal placement to be edited without regenerating the content. Building on these discoveries, we propose \textbf{TimeSteer}, a training-free framework that localizes each utterance's source span through \textbf{Source Span Localization} and transfers the associated audio-visual latent content from the source interval to the specified target interval through \textbf{Region-Aware Latent Remapping}. We further introduce \textbf{SpeechShift}, the first benchmark for interval-level speech scheduling in joint audio-visual generation. Experiments across two representative backbones show that TimeSteer substantially improves interval controllability over training-free baselines while maintaining competitive overall generation quality.

</details>


### [3] [A Cone-Constrained Bilinear Decomposition for Total Scaled-Gradient Variation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00036)
*Haibin Su,Chunlin Wu,Huibin Chang,Zhifang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于双线性分解的TSGV正则化器优化方法，通过将非线性加权梯度解耦为锥或球约束的等价优化问题，结合交替最小化方法和MM策略，实现了具有全局收敛保证的保边保角图像复原，并在高斯去噪和NLOS成像中取得优于或媲美现有变分方法的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TSGV正则化器虽然能很好地保持图像边缘和角点，但其高度非凸非线性的特性带来严重的计算挑战——现有方法要么对参数敏感，要么缺乏收敛保证，亟需一种既有理论收敛保证又计算高效的求解方案。

Method: 提出定制的双线性分解来解耦TSGV正则化器中的非线性加权梯度，将问题转化为由锥或球约束（取决于缩放函数选择）控制的等价优化问题；采用配备MM策略的交替最小化方法求解，无需步长调参即可保证能量单调递减；在Kurdyka-Łojasiewicz框架下建立了全局收敛性分析，并通过约束的渐近行为对保边性质给出了几何解释。

Result: 在高斯去噪和非视域（NLOS）成像的大量数值实验中，所提方法在PSNR和SSIM指标上与代表性变分方法相当或更优，尤其在高噪声水平下表现突出，并在稠密和稀疏扫描下改善了结构重建质量。

Conclusion: 该工作通过巧妙的双线性分解将TSGV正则化的非凸非线性难题转化为可用AMM高效求解的约束优化问题，在保证收敛性的同时实现了优越的保边保角性能，为TSGV在实际图像复原任务中的推广应用提供了坚实的方法论基础。

Abstract: The total scaled-gradient variation (TSGV) regularizer, derived from sparse modeling of piecewise-linear structures, has been shown to preserve edges and corners in image restoration. However, its highly nonconvex and nonlinear nature poses severe computational challenges, as existing methods often suffer from parameter sensitivity or lack convergence guarantees. To overcome this, we propose a tailored bilinear decomposition that decouples the nonlinear weighted gradient in the TSGV regularizer. This approach yields an equivalent optimization problem governed by cone or sphere constraints, depending on the chosen scaling function. In particular, the cone constraint plays a central role in characterizing edge- and corner-preserving behavior. We solve this reformulation using the alternating minimization method (AMM) equipped with a majorization--minimization strategy, ensuring a monotonic decrease in energy without step-size tuning. Furthermore, we provide a geometric interpretation of the edge-preserving properties of these constraints by analyzing their asymptotic behavior near image singularities. We establish the global convergence of the proposed method to a critical point within the Kurdyka--Łojasiewicz framework. Extensive numerical experiments on Gaussian denoising and non-line-of-sight (NLOS) imaging show that the proposed method achieves PSNR and SSIM competitive with or superior to representative variational methods, especially at high noise levels, and improves the structural reconstruction under dense and sparse scanning.

</details>


### [4] [Qwen-Drive-1.0: An Initial Step towards a Vision-Language Foundation Model for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00111)
*Xin Zhou,Zongchuang Zhao,Zhibo Yang,Mingsheng Li,Humen Zhong,Shuai Bai,Du Chu,Ruizhe Chen,Zhaohai Li,Jun Tang,Qiuyue Wang,Mingkun Yang,Jiazhao Zhang,Dayiheng Liu,Dingkang Liang,Xiang Bai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Qwen-Drive-1.0是一个面向自动驾驶的视觉语言基础模型，在保留预训练VLM架构的同时，通过外部BEV感知头和规划专家模块，在统一框架内集成了3D感知、视觉问答和运动规划能力，并通过分阶段训练策略兼顾驾驶专业技能与通用视觉语言能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶需要理解复杂的3D场景并进行安全规划，但目前缺少能够同时具备通用视觉语言理解能力和专精驾驶任务的统一基础模型。作者旨在弥合通用VLM与驾驶专用模型之间的鸿沟。

Method: 保留预训练VLM架构，集成三部分：1) 外部BEV感知头，联合执行3D目标检测、语义占据预测和BEV地图分割，作为共享表征中3D信息的探针及可检查的3D场景接口；2) Planning Expert模块基于共享VLM表征生成未来自车轨迹；3) 分阶段训练策略，将驾驶监督与通用视觉语言数据结合，在获取驾驶能力的同时保持通用视觉理解和指令遵循能力。

Result: 实验表明该模型在3D感知和驾驶场景理解上表现强劲，同时基本保留了通用视觉语言能力；在开放环、伪闭环和闭环三种评估设置下的运动规划性能均具有高度竞争力。

Conclusion: Qwen-Drive-1.0验证了在统一框架内以单一预训练VLM为主体同时实现3D感知、视觉问答和运动规划的可行性，为构建自动驾驶视觉语言基础模型迈出了初步一步。

Abstract: We present Qwen-Drive-1.0, an initial step towards a vision-language foundation model for autonomous driving. Qwen-Drive-1.0 retains the architecture of the pretrained vision-language model (VLM) and integrates 3D perception, visual question answering, and motion planning within a unified framework. An external bird's-eye-view (BEV) perception head jointly performs 3D object detection, semantic occupancy prediction, and BEV map segmentation. It serves as a probe of the 3D information accessible from the shared representations and provides an explicit, inspectable interface to 3D scene structure. A Planning Expert conditions on shared VLM representations to generate future ego trajectories. A staged training recipe combines driving supervision with general-purpose vision-language data to acquire driving-specific competence while helping preserve broad visual understanding and instruction-following capabilities. Experiments demonstrate strong 3D perception and driving scene understanding while largely preserving general vision-language capability. Comprehensive evaluations across open-loop, pseudo-closed-loop, and closed-loop settings further show highly competitive motion-planning performance.

</details>


### [5] [ZimaBlue: Evolving Generalizable World Action Models through Scalable Video Pre-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00188)
*Xionghao Wu,Yijun Yang,Shiyang Zhou,Haoze Sun,Jianhui Liu,Songsong Yu,Jiyao Zhang,Wenbo Li,Bo Wang,Guoqing Ma,Lin Song,Renjie Liao,Shenghe Zheng,Wei Tang,Xiaojuan Qi,Yanwei Li,Yuan Zhang,Zhuotao Tian,Haoyang Huang,Nan Duan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ZimaBlue是一个从大规模视频中学习通用世界动作模型(WAMs)的可扩展框架，通过三阶段训练课程（因果具身视频预训练、视频-动作中期训练、目标机器人特化）以及异步Slow-Fast双系统架构，实现实时机器人控制。从目标机器人数据扩展到12万小时的具身视频后，零样本成功率从36.1%提升至77.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操控面临根本性的扩展挑战：鲁棒泛化需要广泛的物理经验，但带动作标签的机器人轨迹采集成本高昂且多样性有限。第一人称视频提供了更可扩展的具身经验来源，但缺少动作标签。核心挑战是如何将丰富的无动作经验转化为有效的机器人控制。

Method: ZimaBlue提出三阶段训练课程：(1) 在大规模人类和机器人第一人称视频上进行因果具身视频预训练；(2) 通过统一的动作表征在异构机器人轨迹上进行视频-动作中期训练，将视觉动态与动作对齐；(3) 针对目标机器人进行特化部署。同时采用异步Slow-Fast双系统架构，高容量Slow世界模型提供泛化的时空表征，轻量Fast分支实现NVIDIA RTX 4090上30Hz的实时动作预测。

Result: 在真实机器人零样本评估中，训练数据从仅目标机器人数据扩展到超过12万小时的具身视频后，成功率从36.1%提升至77.8%。在多个基准测试上表现强劲，在未见任务上的提升尤为显著。

Conclusion: ZimaBlue证明了大规模无动作视频能够有效转化为通用机器人控制能力，通过分阶段训练和Slow-Fast双系统架构解决了数据扩展性与实时控制之间的权衡。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation faces a fundamental scaling challenge: robust generalization demands broad physical experience, yet action-labeled robot trajectories are expensive to collect and inherently limited in diversity. Egocentric videos offer a far more scalable source of embodied experience, capturing object interactions, contact dynamics, tool use, and long-horizon behaviors across diverse environments. The central challenge is how to convert this abundant but action-free experience into effective robot control. We introduce ZimaBlue, a scalable framework for learning generalizable World Action Models (WAMs) from large-scale video. ZimaBlue follows a three-stage training curriculum: it first performs causal embodied video pre-training on large-scale human and robot egocentric videos, then grounds the learned visual dynamics in heterogeneous robot trajectories through video-action mid-training with a unified action representation, and finally specializes the model to a target robot for deployment. To make generative WAMs practical for real-time control, ZimaBluefurther adopts an asynchronous Slow-Fast dual-system architecture, where a high-capacity Slow world model provides generalizable spatiotemporal representations and a lightweight Fast branch enables 30 Hz action prediction on NVIDIA RTX 4090. On real-robot zero-shot evaluations, scaling from target-robot data alone to over 120,000 hours of embodied video improves success from 36.1% to 77.8%. ZimaBlue further delivers strong performance across multiple benchmarks, with particularly pronounced gains on unseen tasks.

</details>


### [6] [A Lagrangian View of Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00198)
*Peyman Milanfar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自下而上的拉格朗日（粒子中心）力学推导方法，替代传统基于最优传输和连续性方程的欧拉宏观视角，来分析显式时间生成模型（如Flow Matching和Rectified Flow）。通过分析连续去噪器的局部泰勒展开，推导出目标身份守恒这一严格不变条件，进而得到拟线性对流PDE。利用特征线法解析求解该PDE，可得到Flow Matching的直线轨迹，并将去噪器的Jacobian识别为轨迹曲率的主要来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有显式时间生成模型（Flow Matching、Rectified Flow）的推导通常采用自上而下的欧拉（Eulerian）视角，即通过最优传输和连续性方程关注概率质量的宏观输运，缺乏粒子层面的微观力学解释。本文旨在提供一种自下而上的拉格朗日力学推导视角，从单个粒子的角度出发，揭示生成轨迹直线性的根本机制。

Method: 从拉格朗日粒子中心视角出发，对连续去噪器进行局部泰勒展开分析，提出最优单步生成所需的目标身份守恒（conservation of target identity）严格不变性条件。在该条件下推导出控制粒子运动的拟线性对流偏微分方程（PDE），并通过特征线法（Method of Characteristics）解析求解该PDE。

Result: 解析求解该PDE可恢复Flow Matching的直线轨迹；同时从几何角度将去噪器的Jacobian识别为轨迹曲率的主要来源，从而直接解释为何直线流能支持大步长采样，以及经验模型为何需要蒸馏来展平相交的特征线。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种新颖的拉格朗日力学视角来理解显式时间生成模型，揭示了生成轨迹直线性的数学本质源于目标身份守恒条件，并将轨迹曲率归因于去噪器Jacobian，为蒸馏和直线化方法提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Modern explicit-time generative models, such as Flow Matching [Lipman et al., 2023] and Rectified Flow [Liu et al., 2023], are typically derived top-down via Optimal Transport and the continuity equation. This standard Eulerian approach focuses on the macroscopic transport of probability mass. In this paper, we present an alternative, bottom-up mechanical derivation grounded in a Lagrangian (particle-centric) perspective. By analyzing the local Taylor expansion of a continuous denoiser, we motivate a strict invariance condition required for optimal, singlestep generation: the conservation of target identity. Enforcing this condition yields a governing quasi-linear advection Partial Differential Equation (PDE). We demonstrate that solving this PDE via the Method of Characteristics analytically yields the straight-line trajectories of Flow Matching. This geometric perspective isolates the Jacobian of the denoiser as the primary source of trajectory curvature, providing a direct mathematical explanation for why straight-line flows enable massive step sizes, and why empirical models require distillation to flatten intersecting characteristics.

</details>


### [7] [Distributed Implicit Harm: A Compositional Safety Blind Spot in MLLM-Based Video Moderation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00206)
*Ruotong Wang,Zihao Zhu,Siwei Lyu,Xin Tao,Baoyuan Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出"分布式隐性危害"(DIH)概念，指由看似安全组件组合而成、需整体解读才能体现有害含义的视频内容。作者开发了多智能体合成框架生成超过9,000个带推理注释的DIH视频，并在30多个多模态大语言模型上评测，发现前沿模型在识别时问和跨通道两类DIH均存在持续且显著的缺陷，真实社交媒体数据也验证了这一失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全数据集和内容审核方法主要依赖局部显式线索（如单一视觉片段或单模态关键词），而多模态大语言模型在面对"每个组件单独无害、组合后有害"的视频时存在盲区。由于这类视频缺少组合性危害标注、难以通过局部特征检索，且未被现有安全数据集覆盖，缺乏规模化研究所需的数据。

Method: 作者提出多智能体合成框架，将各自良性的组件组合为危害性场景，生成多样化的DIH视频并附带显式推理注释。研究覆盖两种代表性分解轴：时间维度上的视觉片段间危害(DIH-T)和音频-视觉跨模态危害(DIH-M)。数据集包含超过9,000个视频，涵盖纯视觉与音视频设置；评测了超过30个多模态大语言模型，包括专有前沿模型和开源模型。

Result: 基准测试显示所有模型在检测DIH-T和DIH-M方面均存在显著且一致的缺陷。最强的前沿模型也常能正确评估单独组件，但无法识别组件组合后涌现的有害含义。在从社交媒体手动收集的真实世界DIH视频上，同样的失败模式依然存在，验证了该问题的实际性与普遍性。

Conclusion: DIH是视频审核中一个实际存在但尚未充分探索的挑战，揭示当前多模态大语言模型在组合性安全推理方面的系统性盲区，亟需更多关注与针对性改进。

Abstract: Despite their growing use in video moderation, multimodal large language models (MLLMs) exhibit a compositional safety blind spot: videos composed of seemingly benign components can convey harmful meaning when interpreted as a whole. We refer to this phenomenon as Distributed Implicit Harm (DIH), where harm arises from relations among components distributed along a decomposition axis of the video, rather than from any single explicit cue. Among many possible axes, we study two representative cases: temporally distributed harm across visual segments (DIH-T) and cross-modal harm between audio and visual streams (DIH-M). Studying and mitigating DIH at scale requires data that is difficult to collect: such videos lack compositional harm annotations, evade retrieval based on local visual cues, keywords, or single-modality signals, and are consequently absent from existing safety datasets. To bridge this gap, we develop a multi-agent synthesis framework that composes individually benign components into harmful scenarios and generates diverse DIH videos with explicit reasoning annotations, yielding a dataset of over 9,000 videos spanning visual-only and audio-visual settings. Benchmarking over 30 MLLMs spanning frontier proprietary models and leading open-source systems reveals substantial and consistent deficits in detecting both DIH-T and DIH-M. Notably, this failure persists even among the strongest frontier models: they often correctly assess individual components in isolation but fail to recognize the harmful meaning that emerges from their composition. We further evaluate these models on a manually collected set of real-world DIH videos from social media and observe the same failure mode, highlighting DIH as a practical and underexplored challenge for video moderation.

</details>


### [8] [Beyond Language Priors: Diagnosing and Fixing Visual-Origin Hallucinations in Multimodal LLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00231)
*Peiyang Xu,Xiaopei Zhu,Jun Zhu,Xiaolin Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文挑战了多模态大语言模型（MLLM）目标幻觉研究中对语言先验的主流归因，提出"视觉起源幻觉"这一互补性原因，即错误视觉特征提取及图文嵌入对齐不足导致的幻觉。基于余弦相似度分析和Smooth Grad-CAM熵测量，作者发现幻觉样本具有系统性更低的图文相似度（0.158 vs -0.122）以及反转的注意力模式。据此提出对抗对比微调（ACFT）方法，通过对抗幻觉属性翻转（AHAF）构造完美对齐的正负样本对进行对比微调，该过程同时可作为诊断探针。仅使用COCO数据集的0.9%且零推理开销，ACFT在POPE、MME和四个描述级幻觉基准上，在LLaVA、MiniGPT-4和Qwen2.5-VL上取得最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将MLLM目标幻觉主要归因于语言先验（如过度依赖文本共现统计），本文认为存在一个被忽视的补充性成因——视觉起源幻觉，即由错误的视觉特征提取和图文嵌入对齐不充分所引发的问题。作者希望从视觉角度定量揭示这一未被充分探索的机制，并据此提出相应解决方案。

Method: 方法包含两个核心部分：（1）诊断分析：通过余弦相似度分析和Smooth Grad-CAM熵测量揭示幻觉样本在视觉-语言对齐上的系统性缺陷；（2）ACFT架构：采用AHAF（对抗幻觉属性翻转）程序，通过最小化且目标明确的对抗扰动翻转图像的幻觉属性，构造完美对齐的正负样本对，用于对比微调。AHAF同时作为诊断探针，揭示MLLM视觉表征紧邻幻觉决策边界。

Result: ACFT仅使用COCO数据集的0.9%数据、零推理开销，即在POPE、MME及四个描述级幻觉基准上，横跨LLaVA、MiniGPT-4和Qwen2.5-VL等多个模型取得最先进（SOTA）性能。此外，诊断分析揭示了幻觉样本图文相似度系统性偏低及注意力模式反转的关键证据。

Conclusion: 本文证明确实存在语言先验之外的、源于视觉特征提取错误的幻觉成因。ACFT通过有效的对抗对比微调大幅缓解了视觉起源幻觉，同时揭示了MLLM视觉表征处于幻觉决策边界附近的危险性，为幻觉检测与缓解提供了新的诊断视角和高效实用的方案。

Abstract: Existing research on object hallucination in multimodal large language models (MLLMs) predominantly attributes the problem to language priors such as over-reliance on textual co-occurrence statistics. We challenge this view by presenting quantitative evidence for a complementary, under-explored cause: visual-origin hallucination, where hallucinations arise from incorrect visual feature extraction and misalignment between image and text embeddings. Through cosine similarity analysis and Smooth Grad-CAM entropy measurements, we show that hallucinated samples exhibit systematically lower image-text similarity (average 0.158 vs. -0.122) and inverted attention patterns, where attention is dispersed when the target object is present but wrongly concentrated when it is absent. Guided by this diagnosis, we propose Adversarial Contrastive Fine-Tuning (ACFT). ACFT uses an Adversarial Hallucination Attribute Flipping (AHAF) procedure, involving minimal, targeted adversarial perturbations that flip an image's hallucination attribute, to construct perfectly aligned positive-negative pairs, which are then used for contrastive fine-tuning. AHAF simultaneously serves as a diagnostic probe, revealing that MLLM visual representations lie dangerously close to hallucination decision boundaries. Requiring only 0.9% of the COCO dataset and adding zero inference overhead, ACFT achieves state-of-the-art performance on POPE, MME, and four description-level hallucination benchmarks across LLaVA, MiniGPT-4, and Qwen2.5-VL. Code is available at https://github.com/zxp555/ACFT_MM

</details>


### [9] [Beyond Blind Compliance: Benchmarking Task Verification in OCR Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00232)
*Yue Zhou,Yuan Wu,Yi Chang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出OCR场景下的"任务验证"问题——模型在回答前应先判断图像前提、文本前提和问题是否构成可执行任务。作者构建了VeriOCRBench基准（1,800个人工验证样本，含1,600个陷阱注入的无效任务及200个无陷阱对照），用于解耦评估任务验证、根因诊断和过度拒答能力。实验显示15个主流MLLM存在持续的盲目服从、诊断失败和提示诱导的过度拒答，暴露了当前OCR推理系统的可靠性缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM在OCR文档理解和文本丰富的视觉推理基准上表现出色，但现有评测大多假设每个任务都是有效且可回答的。真实OCR场景中这一假设经常不成立：问题可能涉及不可读文本、被遮挡的证据、不存在的视觉目标、矛盾的先决条件或缺失的变量。这种"可靠性缺口"尚未被系统性研究。

Method: 1) 提出OCR-grounded Task Verification框架，要求模型在回答前判断图像前提、文本前提和问题三者是否共同定义了一个可执行任务；2) 构建VeriOCRBench基准，从8个OCR相关数据集抽取源图像，覆盖8个真实图像域，包含1,600个跨8种陷阱类型和四个验证维度（视觉、上下文、事实、逻辑）的无效任务，以及200个无陷阱对照；3) 采用视觉原子事实（VAF）锚定管线构建，并经过完整人工审计；4) 对15个主流MLLM进行解耦评估。

Result: 15个主流MLLM的评估揭示了持续的盲目服从（模型忽略陷阱仍作答）、根因诊断失败（模型无法正确识别任务无效的原因）以及提示诱导的过度拒答（在有效任务上不必要地拒绝回答），验证了当前OCR推理系统存在严重的可靠性缺口。

Conclusion: 现有MLLM在OCR推理中缺乏任务验证能力，面临可靠性缺口。VeriOCRBench提供了首个支持任务验证、根因诊断与过度拒答三者解耦评测的基准，为提升OCR场景下的鲁棒性与安全性提供了重要评估工具。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have achieved strong performance on OCR-centric document understanding and text-rich visual reasoning benchmarks. Yet existing evaluations largely assume that every task is valid and answerable. In real-world OCR scenarios, this assumption often fails: questions may rely on illegible text, occluded evidence, nonexistent visual targets, contradictory premises, or missing variables. We study this reliability gap as OCR-grounded Task Verification: before answering, a model should determine whether the Image Premise (IP), Textual Premise (TP), and Question (Q) jointly define an executable task.
  We introduce VeriOCRBench, a 1,800-sample human-verified benchmark built from source images drawn from 8 OCR-related datasets and spanning 8 real-world image domains, with controlled, image-grounded diagnostic tasks.
  It contains 1,600 trap-injected invalid tasks across 8 trap types and four verification dimensions---Visual, Contextual, Factual, and Logical---plus 200 trap-free controls for measuring over-refusal. Built with a Visual Atomic Fact (VAF)-anchored pipeline and full human auditing, VeriOCRBench enables decoupled evaluation of task verification, root-cause diagnosis, and over-refusal. Evaluating 15 leading MLLMs reveals persistent blind compliance, diagnosis failures, and prompt-induced over-refusal, exposing a critical reliability gap in current OCR reasoning systems.
  The code is available at: https://github.com/zy001122/Beyond-Blind-Compliance.

</details>


### [10] [CoLT-Drive: Counterfactual Long-Tail Benchmarking and Knowledge-Preserving Adaptation for Driving Affordance Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00242)
*Zhengxu Tang,Guofeng Cui,Ziyu Gong,Xiaozhou Zhang,Ruifeng Deng,Chengzhi Qi,Ke Chen,Sachin Patil,Tianjun Xiao,Langechuan Liu,Pichao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出自动驾驶长尾问题不应仅视为罕见物体识别错误，而应关注模型能否推断罕见物体如何改变自车可行的高层驾驶动作。作者将其形式化为决策级驾驶可供性预测任务，并引入CoLT-Drive反事实长尾基准（3,536个样本，将罕见物体插入固定驾驶场景），以及KPA知识保留适应框架，在CoLT-Drive上达到60.8%的配对准确率，优于预训练基线（50.3%）与LoRA SFT（32.4%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点将自动驾驶长尾失败视为罕见物体识别问题，但作者认为这不完整：决策关键问题不仅在于模型能否识别异常物体，更在于能否推断该物体如何改变自车可行的高层动作集合。因此需要新的任务定义与评估基准来度量这一能力。

Method: 方法包含两部分：(1) 任务与基准——将问题形式化为决策级驾驶可供性预测（从前视图图像、自车运动历史与导航指令映射到纵向-横向结构元动作），构建CoLT-Drive反事实长尾基准；(2) 模型框架KPA——结合结构化感知到决策提示、SLERP专家合并和RegMoE（基于驾驶决策状态的LoRA混合专家模块），在保留下游部署小型VLM的开放世界知识的同时，将轻量适配能力分配到不同驾驶决策状态域。

Result: 在域内驾驶划分与CoLT-Drive基准上的实验显示：KPA在CoLT-Drive上达到60.8%配对准确率，显著优于预训练Qwen3-VL-2B基线（50.3%）和LoRA SFT（32.4%），同时保持有竞争力的域内准确率。

Conclusion: 长尾驾驶安全应聚焦于决策级可供性推断而非仅物体识别；所提出的CoLT-Drive基准与KPA框架可有效提升小型VLM在反事实长尾场景下的驾驶决策能力，同时不牺牲预训练开放世界知识。

Abstract: Long-tail autonomous driving failures are often framed as rare-object recognition errors. We argue that this view is incomplete: the decision-critical question is not only whether a model recognizes an unusual object, but whether it infers how that object changes the ego vehicle's feasible high-level actions. We formalize this problem as decision-level driving affordance prediction, where a model maps a front-view image, ego-motion history, and navigation command to a structured longitudinal--lateral meta-action. To evaluate this capability, we introduce CoLT-Drive, a 3,536-sample counterfactual long-tail benchmark that inserts rare objects into otherwise fixed driving scenes and measures whether models predict acceptable action pairs. To improve deployable small VLMs, we propose KPA, a knowledge-preserving adaptation framework that combines structured perception-to-decision prompting, SLERP-based expert merging, and RegMoE, a regime-aware LoRA mixture-of-experts module. KPA preserves the pretrained model's open-world knowledge while allocating lightweight adaptation capacity to different driving decision regimes. Experiments on an in-domain driving split and CoLT-Drive show that KPA achieves 60.8\% pair accuracy on CoLT-Drive, outperforming the pretrained Qwen3-VL-2B baseline (50.3\%) and LoRA SFT (32.4\%) while maintaining competitive in-domain accuracy. Our benchmark and code are available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/tangzx2024/CoLT-Drive and https://github.com/tangzhengxu/CoLT-Drive.

</details>


### [11] [CrossFeat: Bridging Imaging Modalities in Feature Descriptor Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00272)
*Paul Schneider,Nazim Haouchine*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CrossFeat框架，使已有的单模态描述子能够在跨模态场景下工作，通过学习描述子空间中的跨域映射函数，并采用几何-外观解耦仅改变外观而保留几何信息，在多个数据集上提升了多模态匹配性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关键点描述子主要针对单模态设置（视角、光照、对比度变化）。多模态场景涉及不同传感过程（多光谱成像、RGB-深度、卫星图像、医学成像等），同一结构在不同模态下外观差异大。已有解决方案要么为每对模态重新训练描述子（模态变化需重训），要么训练大型模型（运行开销大），缺乏高效且通用的跨模态描述方案。

Method: CrossFeat框架学习一个描述子空间中的crossing函数，将一种模态的特征映射为与另一种模态兼容的表示。核心创新是引入几何-外观解耦（geometry-appearance disentanglement），在映射过程中只改变外观信息而完整保留几何属性，从而维持原始描述子捕捉的结构信息。该方法无需为每对模态重新训练描述子，也不需要大型模型。

Result: 在多个领域和数据集上的实验表明，CrossFeat在多模态匹配任务中取得了 improved performance，相比重新训练每个模态对或使用大型模型的方法具有更好的效率和泛化性。

Conclusion: CrossFeat通过几何-外观解耦的跨域映射，使现有单模态描述子无需重训即可扩展到多模态场景，在保持效率的同时提升了多模态匹配性能，为跨模态关键点描述提供了一个轻量且通用的解决方案。

Abstract: Most advances in keypoint descriptions address monomodal settings, where image variations arise from viewpoint, illumination, or contrast changes. Multimodal scenarios involve images produced by fundamentally different sensing processes, such as multispectral imaging, RGB-depth, satellite imagery, or medical imaging, causing the same structures to appear differently. A common solution to cross-modal description is to train descriptors for each modality pair, which requires retraining whenever the modalities change, or to train large models, which incur a significant increase in runtime. Instead, we propose CrossFeat, a framework that enables an existing monomodal descriptor to operate across modalities. Our method learns a crossing function in descriptor space that maps features from one modality to a representation compatible with another. To preserve the structural information captured by the original descriptor, CrossFeat introduces a geometry-appearance disentanglement such that only appearance is altered while the geometric properties are preserved. Experiments across multiple domains and datasets demonstrate improved performance in multimodal matching.

</details>


### [12] [StreamScout: Learning When to Look Deeper for Streaming Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00291)
*Ce Zhang,Jing Bi,Jinxi He,Jianshu Zhang,Jingyang Lin,Yunzhong Xiao,Minghao Fu,Yaqi Xie,Zhentao Xie,Weicong Chen,Katia Sycara,Ming Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出StreamScout，一个自适应推理框架，用于流式视频理解。其核心思想是：不仅决定记忆存储什么重要，按查询动态决定记忆访问深度同样重要。框架维护轻量文本时间线，在查询时渐进式地添加三种视觉视图（近期帧快照、均匀回看、查询相关检索），逐级判断证据是否充分，不够则升级。通过蒸馏和强化学习优化"停止或升级"策略，显著降低推理成本和token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流式视频理解系统主要关注在有限记忆中保留什么，但对于每个查询都使用相同的固定成本访问记忆，忽略了不同查询所需证据的差异。作者认为，对每个查询决定记忆访问的深度与决定记忆存储的内容同等重要，现有方法缺乏这种按查询自适应的推理机制。

Method: StreamScout采用自适应推理框架：1）在流展开时仅维护轻量文本时间线；2）查询时渐进式增补三种视觉视图（近期帧快照、均匀回看、查询显著检索）逐级递进；3）每阶段若证据充足立即作答，否则升级到下一视图；4）通过辅助集上探测级联并蒸馏模型的经验能力边界作为监督信号，训练轻量LoRA适配（StreamScout-S）；5）进一步用强化学习优化停止策略，使模型能探索超出蒸馏决策的停止行为（StreamScout-R）。

Result: 在三种骨干网络和三个流式基准上，StreamScout及变体一致优于现有流式方法，同时大幅降低推理成本和token消耗。例如在OVO-Bench上，StreamScout-S将Qwen3-VL-8B提升14.65分，同时比均匀采样节省59%的token，平均作答时间仅1.04秒。

Conclusion: 在流式视频理解中，按查询自适应的记忆访问深度至关重要。StreamScout通过渐进式视觉视图与可学习停止策略，在不牺牲准确率的前提下显著降低推理成本，验证了"按需取证、逐级升级"这一推理范式的有效性。

Abstract: Streaming video understanding requires answering questions that arrive at arbitrary moments over an unbounded video stream. Existing systems primarily focus on what to retain in a bounded memory, yet access that memory using the same fixed-cost procedure for every query, despite substantial variation in the evidence required. We argue that deciding how deeply to access memory for each query is as important as deciding what the memory should store. To this end, we introduce StreamScout, an adaptive inference framework that maintains only a lightweight textual timeline in context as the stream unfolds. At query time, StreamScout progressively augments the timeline with up to three increasingly informative visual views: a glance at recent frames, a uniform look-back over the past stream, and query-salient retrieval. At each stage, the model answers immediately if the available evidence is sufficient; otherwise, it escalates to the next view. To improve this stop-or-escalate policy, we probe the cascade on an auxiliary set and distill the model's empirical competence boundary into supervision for a lightweight LoRA adaptation, yielding StreamScout-S. We further refine the policy through reinforcement learning, allowing the model to explore stopping behaviors beyond imitation of the distilled decisions, yielding StreamScout-R. Across three backbones and three streaming benchmarks, StreamScout and its variants consistently outperform prior streaming methods while substantially reducing inference cost and token consumption; on OVO-Bench, for instance, StreamScout-S improves Qwen3-VL-8B by 14.65 points while using 59% fewer tokens than uniform sampling and answering in 1.04 s on average.

</details>


### [13] [TRUST: Threshold-Recalibrated Uncertainty-Safe Training for Certified Dismissal in Breast Cancer Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00300)
*Parham Hajishafiezahramini,Matthew Hamilton,Edward Kendall,Gregory Doyle,Oscar Meruvia Pastor*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种闭环阈值感知训练策略用于乳腺X光筛查，在训练过程中动态重算淘汰阈值并惩罚接近淘汰区域的癌症阳性图像，在NLBS和RSNA数据集上实现更高的淘汰率同时保持98%和95%的目标召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 减少对明确无癌症（cancer-negative）筛查性乳腺X光片的复阅可以降低放射科医生工作负荷而不影响癌症检出率。作者希望通过训练策略改进实现高召回率下更安全的病例排除。

Method: 提出闭环阈值感知训练策略（closed-loop threshold-aware training），训练过程中动态重算淘汰阈值（dismissal threshold），并使用该阈值对接近淘汰区域的癌症阳性图像施加惩罚。

Result: 在NLBS和RSNA上采用五种受控训练配置评估，基于单侧99% Clopper-Pearson上界估计被淘汰病例中的癌症患病率。模型在98%和95%召回目标下均取得最高病例级淘汰率：NLBS上达19.74%和21.70%，交叉熵基线未达到召回目标；RSNA上淘汰率从7.04%提升至14.31%、从13.49%提升至19.69%。外部RSNA→NLBS评估中，98%和95%召回目标下淘汰率分别为12.95%和19.87%。

Conclusion: 实验结果支持闭环阈值感知训练策略在高召回选择性淘汰任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Reducing the review of clearly cancer-negative screening mammograms could lower radiologist workload without compromising cancer detection. We propose a closed-loop threshold-aware training strategy in which the dismissal threshold is recalculated during training and used to penalize cancer-positive images that approach the dismissal region. We evaluated the method on NLBS and RSNA using five controlled training configurations, with case-level assessment based on a one-sided 99\% Clopper--Pearson upper bound for cancer prevalence among dismissed cases. The proposed model achieved the highest case-level dismissal rates at both 98\% and 95\% recall targets. On NLBS, dismissal reached 19.74\% and 21.70\%, while the cross-entropy baseline did not meet either recall target. On RSNA, dismissal improved from 7.04\% to 14.31\% and from 13.49\% to 19.69\%. In external RSNA$\to$NLBS evaluation, the proposed model achieved dismissal rates of 12.95\% and 19.87\% at the 98\% and 95\% recall targets, respectively. These results support closed-loop threshold-aware training for high-recall selective dismissal.

</details>


### [14] [Where Should Experience Live? Hierarchical Hebbian Memory for Continual Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00358)
*Mohammed Yusuf Mujawar,Noorbakhsh Amiri Golilarz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Hierarchical Hebbian Memory（HHM），一种集成在Vision Transformer内部的三级记忆架构，包括快速工作记忆、持久路由情景记忆和慢速语义记忆，通过可学习控制器进行调节，并采用因果的先读后写生命周期防止数据泄漏。在Omniglot 5-way 1-shot和CORe50任务上分别达到97.39%和95.37%的准确率，显著优于无记忆或单层记忆的基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vision Transformer虽然能提供强视觉表征，但其参数更新缓慢，无法有效组织不同时间尺度下的新获取信息。现有方法缺少能够在快速、持久和巩固三种时间尺度上组织在线视觉经验的记忆机制。

Method: 提出三级记忆架构：Rapid Working Memory（快速工作记忆）、Persistent Routed Episodic Memory（持久路由情景记忆）和Slower Semantic Memory（慢速语义记忆）。可学习控制器统一调节记忆贡献、读写路由、可塑性、保留和巩固。采用因果的先读后写生命周期设计，确保当前输入的结果不会影响该输入监督下的预测。

Result: 基于Swin-Tiny，Omniglot上达到97.39%准确率，CORe50上结合经验回放达到95.37%最终准确率。多存储库检索的延迟关联准确率达47.50%，远高于单一持久存储库的24.17%和无记忆的25.00%。在干扰项插入后，情景记忆保持约0.96的余弦相似度，而工作记忆降至约0.05。

Conclusion: Hebbian式联想与可学习记忆路由机制能够共同组织Vision Transformer中的在线视觉经验，跨越快速、持久和巩固三种记忆时间尺度，证明了分层记忆集成对持续学习与少样本识别的有效性。

Abstract: Vision Transformers provide strong visual representations but typically rely on slowly updated parameters, limiting their ability to organize newly acquired information across different memory timescales. This work proposes \textit{Hierarchical Hebbian Memory}, a three-level memory architecture composed of rapid Working Memory, persistent Routed Episodic Memory, and slower Semantic Memory. A learned controller regulates memory contribution, read and write routing, plasticity, retention, and consolidation. A causal read-before-write lifecycle ensures that the current outcome cannot influence the prediction it supervises. The architecture is evaluated on Omniglot 5-way 1-shot recognition and CORe50 continual object recognition. With Swin-Tiny, the hierarchical model reaches 97.39\% accuracy on Omniglot and 95.37\% final accuracy on CORe50 when combined with experience replay. Learned multi-bank retrieval reaches 47.50\% delayed-association accuracy, compared with 24.17\% for a single persistent bank and 25.00\% without memory. After intervening distractors, Episodic Memory retains approximately 0.96 cosine similarity with stored associations, while Working Memory falls to approximately 0.05. These results show that Hebbian association and learned memory routing can jointly organize online visual experience across rapid, persistent, and consolidated memory timescales within Vision Transformers.

</details>


### [15] [Puppeteer: Object-Grounded Posture-Aware Co-Speech Gesture Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00369)
*Vida Adeli,Soroush Mehraban,Jacob Rommann,Harrison Sanborn,Cole Clifford,Babak Taati*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Puppeteer，一种姿态感知、物体锚定的共语手势扩散模型，在因果潜空间中实现与语音对齐、与周围物体物理一致的手势合成，并引入新评估指标和SceneGes合成数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的语音驱动手势生成模型过度强调音频与手势的对齐，但未显式考虑姿态约束或周围物体，未能捕捉身体手势与物理空间之间的内在关联，导致生成的手势缺乏物理一致性。

Method: 1) 将长手势分解为结构化动作基元，学习因果变分自编码器编码为时间有序的因果潜在token（仅依赖过去）；2) 在因果潜空间中进行条件扩散，以语音信号、运动历史、初始姿态参考和物体几何为条件，合成物理一致的手势；3) 提出新的评估指标并构建SceneGes合成3D数据集。

Result: 实验表明Puppeteer生成的手势比此前方法更多样且时间同步性更好，同时支持物体锚定的手势合成，并支持手势插值（in-betweening）和手势补全等任务。

Conclusion: 时间有序的因果潜在表征使显式时序控制成为可能，是连接姿态约束、物体几何与语音信号进行共语手势生成的有效方案，为物体锚定手势合成提供了新范式。

Abstract: Generating co-speech gestures that are temporally coherent, semantically aligned with speech, and grounded with surrounding objects remains challenging. Prior speech-driven gesture models emphasize audio-gesture alignment but do not explicitly account for posture constraints or surrounding objects, failing to capture the inherent correlation between body gestures and the physical space. We present Puppeteer, a posture-aware, object-grounded co-speech gesture diffusion model operating in a causal latent space. We decompose long gestures into structured primitives and learn a causal variational autoencoder that encodes them into temporally ordered latent tokens, each depending only on the past. We then perform conditional diffusion directly in the causal latent space, conditioning on speech signals, motion history, an initial posture reference, and object geometry to synthesize physically consistent gestures. This temporally ordered latent formulation enables explicit temporal control and supports tasks such as gesture in-betweening and gesture completion. To better assess co-speech gesture synthesis beyond existing measures, we introduce new evaluation metrics tailored to this task. We also created SceneGes, the first curated synthetic 3D dataset of embodied co-speech gestures and corresponding 3D objects, enabling object-grounded gesture generation. Experiments show that Puppeteer generates more diverse and temporally synchronized gestures than prior methods, while enabling object-grounded gesture synthesis.

</details>


### [16] [FoldingAgent: Inferring Parametric Origami Procedures from Demonstration Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00377)
*Maya Moriya,Sigal Raab,Yael Vinker,Tali Dekel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了FoldingAgent，一个智能体框架，利用预训练VLM的推理能力并配备专用工具，直接从折纸演示视频中推断出显式的参数化折叠程序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类折纸知识主要通过非结构化的视觉演示传播，而计算方法通常依赖结构化、参数化表示（如折痕图或可执行参数化方案），两者之间存在鸿沟。现有预测静态折痕图案的模型无法捕捉多步折叠的动态过程，且容易产生累积误差。

Method: 定义了一个参数化空间，包括纸张几何结构和一组参数化折叠动作。利用预训练VLM作为核心推理引擎，配备一套专用工具（模拟几何变换、验证物理可行性、检索和比较视觉内容、自我评估预测）。智能体顺序操作，具备重新规划动作的能力。

Result: 在PurelandFold基准（新整理的多樣Pureland折纸视频数据集，含真实几何和动作标签）上评估，成功展示了将VLM推理与专用工具和物理模拟相结合，能够将非结构化视觉演示转换为可执行、物理上合理的折叠流程。

Conclusion: 通过推理智能体和工具辅助的方式，有效弥合了人类视觉折纸知识与计算方法之间的差距，解决了多步折叠中的累积误差问题。

Abstract: We present FoldingAgent, an agentic framework for inferring explicit parametric folding programs directly from origami demonstration videos. Our framework leverages the reasoning power of a pre-trained Vision-Language Model (VLM) equipped with a suite of specialized tools that enable the agent to simulate geometric transitions, verify physical plausibility, retrieve and compare visual content, and evaluate its own predictions. To translate visual content into folding programs, we define a parametric space that consists of the paper's geometry and a set of parametric folding actions. Unlike models that predict static crease patterns, our agent operates sequentially and possesses the ability to re-plan its actions, effectively mitigating the compounding errors inherent in multi-step folding. Our approach takes a step toward closing the gap between human origami knowledge, which is primarily shared through unstructured visual demonstrations, and computational methods, which typically rely on structured, parametric representations such as a crease pattern or an executable parametric plan. We evaluate our approach on PurelandFold, a newly curated benchmark of diverse Pureland origami videos with ground-truth geometry and action labels. Our results demonstrate that by combining VLM reasoning with a set of specialized tools and physical simulation, we can successfully transform unstructured visual demonstrations into executable, physically plausible folding procedures.

</details>


### [17] [SlideMix: Enhancing Whole Slide Image Analysis via Multimodal Shuffling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00396)
*Chad Wong,Sicheng Chen,Tianyi Zhang,Enhui Chai,Yueming Jin,Zeyu Liu,Fei Xia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SlideMix是一个模型无关的多模态数据增强框架，用于基于多实例学习（MIL）的病理全切片图像（WSI）分析。它利用检索增强的视觉语言模型（VLM）选择诊断相关区域、执行原位瓦片洗牌进行特征混合、通过VLM软标签模块监督混合样本，并采用多因素损失驱动的在线课程学习反馈机制自适应控制洗牌粒度。在11个WSI数据集、20,523张切片、8个诊断任务和10个WSI骨干网络上验证了其优越的准确性和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WSI分析面临千兆像素规模、组织异质性、弱切片级监督、稀疏诊断区域和多尺度证据等挑战。现有MIL方法中的数据增强策略常扰动组织区域，未能保持诊断相关性、切片上下文和跨尺度结构，亟需一种能保留诊断意义并促进鲁棒表示学习的增强方法。

Method: SlideMix由四个关键组件构成：(1) 基于检索增强VLM的视觉-语言自适应区域选择器（Visual-Language Adaptive Region selector），用于识别诊断相关区域并减少弱标签噪声；(2) 原位瓦片洗牌（In-place Tile Shuffling）机制，在有意义组织区域内混合特征嵌入以保留切片级上下文；(3) VLM软标签模块对混合样本进行监督；(4) 多因子、损失驱动的在线课程学习反馈方案（Curriculum-Learning Feedback），自适应控制洗牌粒度、特征相似性和洗牌比例，促进跨尺度表示学习。

Result: 在11个WSI数据集、20,523张切片、8个诊断任务和10个WSI骨干网络上的广泛实验中，SlideMix在大多数设置下提升了准确率和泛化性能，并与已建立的增强基线方法相比表现更优，作为即插即用的方法展现出良好效果。

Conclusion: SlideMix提供了一种简单、即插即用的数据增强方案，能够有效地提升数字病理学模型的鲁棒性和可扩展性，为MIL-based WSI分析提供了新的增强范式，代码已开源。

Abstract: Histopathological whole slide images (WSIs) are central to cancer diagnosis, but their gigapixel scale, tissue heterogeneity, weak slide-level supervision, sparse diagnostic regions, and multi-scale evidence make robust automated analysis challenging. Multiple instance learning (MIL) is widely used to aggregate tile-level features into slide-level predictions, yet existing augmentation strategies often perturb tissue regions without preserving diagnostic relevance, slide context, or cross-scale structure. We propose SlideMix, a model-agnostic multimodal augmentation framework for MIL-based WSI analysis. SlideMix uses a retrieval-augmented vision-language model (VLM)-based Visual-Language Adaptive Region selector to identify diagnostically relevant regions and reduce weak-label noise. It then performs In-place Tile Shuffling within meaningful tissue regions to mix feature embeddings while preserving slide-level context. A VLM-based soft-labeling module supervises mixed samples, while a multi-factor, loss-driven online Curriculum-Learning Feedback scheme adaptively controls shuffle granularity, feature similarity, and shuffle ratio to promote cross-scale representation learning. Across 11 WSI datasets comprising 20,523 slides, 8 diagnostic tasks, and 10 WSI backbones, SlideMix improves accuracy and generalization in most settings and compares favorably with established augmentation baselines, providing a simple plug-and-play approach for more robust and scalable digital pathology models. Source code: https://github.com/Xia-Research-Lab/SlideMix

</details>


### [18] [Unmasking Face Embeddings: Reading, Rendering and Naming with Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00411)
*Fizza Rubab,Yiying Tong,Arun Ross*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过简单的预计算线性变换，将领域特定的人脸识别模型嵌入与基础模型（如视觉-语言基础模型）对齐，使人脸嵌入无需训练或修改任何模型即可实现自然语言查询、面部图像重建和身份命名等多模态交互能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人脸识别模型产生的身份嵌入虽然对生物特征匹配高度有效，但缺乏语义可解释性；而基础模型提供了丰富的视觉内容描述、检索和生成接口。作者希望弥合两者之间的鸿沟，探索域特定人脸嵌入与通用基础模型的互操作性所能带来的新能力。

Method: 基于嵌入兼容性的已有工作，仅利用成对嵌入数据估计简单的预计算线性变换，将现有人脸识别模型输出的身份嵌入映射到现成基础模型的嵌入空间中。整个过程无需训练或修改任何模型，对齐后可进行三种应用：自然语言自由文本查询人脸嵌入图库、通过扩散解码器将嵌入渲染为人脸图像、将嵌入转换为名字以完成身份识别。

Result: 仅通过一个线性变换，人脸身份嵌入即可转化为与Web级基础模型兼容的丰富嵌入，支持自然语言查询、人脸图像重建和身份命名等多种下游能力。

Conclusion: 这种互操作性揭示人脸嵌入是语义和视觉上丰富的生物特征表示，对可解释性、检索、重建和模板安全具有直接影响，同时为模板安全带来了新的讨论维度。

Abstract: Modern face recognition (FR) owes much of its success to deep neural networks that learn to extract compact identity embeddings from face images. These models are typically trained for identity discrimination, producing embeddings that are highly effective for biometric matching but largely opaque to semantic interpretation. In contrast, foundation models, pretrained on broad visual or vision--language tasks, provide rich interfaces for describing, retrieving, generating, and organizing visual content. This contrast raises a natural question: what capabilities become available when face embeddings from domain-specific FR models are made interoperable with foundation models? Building on recent work on embedding compatibility across models, we use simple pre-computed linear transformations, estimated from paired embeddings alone, to connect existing FR models with off-the-shelf foundation models. Once aligned with a foundation model, a face embedding can be 'unmasked' in multiple ways, without training or modifying either model: it can be read in natural language, enabling free-form text queries over a gallery of FR embeddings; rendered into a face image that recovers a person's appearance, using an unmodified diffusion decoder; and converted to a name, enabling identification even in the absence of an enrolled face gallery. In effect, one linear transformation turns an identity embedding into a rich embedding for web-scale foundation models. This interoperability exposes face embeddings as semantically and visually rich biometric representations, with direct implications for interpretability, retrieval, reconstruction, and template security.

</details>


### [19] [Instance-Guided Report Anchoring for Text-Free 3D Abnormality Segmentation in Chest CT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00447)
*Zhenyu Bu,Haoyan Ding,Chushu Shen,Xinyuan Zheng,Peiyu Duan,Xueqi Guo,Sepehr Farhand,Yoshihisa Shinagawa,Gerardo Hermosillo,Chaowei Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出IGRA模块，通过将每个标注异常实例池化并锚定到对应报告发现嵌入来建立实例-文本对应关系，推理时丢弃所有文本组件，实现仅图像输入的多标签3D异常分割，显著优于纯图像基线且可与需要报告引导的方法相媲美。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胸部CT中精确的3D异常分割需要密集体素级标注，获取成本高昂；而放射报告作为临床常规产物包含实例级描述，可作为免费监督信号，但现有视觉-语言定位方法推理时依赖成对文本且每次仅能定位单个查询发现，实用性受限。

Method: 提出实例引导报告锚定（IGRA），一种模型无关模块：训练时将每个标注异常实例表示池化并锚定到描述该实例的对应报告发现嵌入，建立实例-文本对应；推理时丢弃全部文本相关组件，仅需图像一次前向即可预测所有异常类别。同时将ReXGroundingCT上的自由文本定位重写为多标签体素分割，合并同类别实例。

Result: IGRA在Dice上较最强纯图像基线提升22.5%（30.93 vs 25.25）；在单发现子集上与VoxTell相当（30.29 vs 30.43）。在四个标准3D分割骨架上均稳定提升Dice和命中率；在LIDC-IDRI、PleThora及私有数据集上的零样本评估均一致优于纯图像基线。

Conclusion: IGRA有效利用临床报告提供的弱监督信号增强3D异常分割，训练阶段建立实例-文本锚定后可完全摆脱推理时对文本的依赖，是一种通用且可迁移至不同分割骨架构架的模型无关方案。

Abstract: Accurate 3D abnormality segmentation in chest CT requires dense spatial supervision, but obtaining expert voxel-level labels is costly. Radiology reports, however, are routinely generated during clinical interpretation and contain instance-specific descriptions that can provide additional guidance without new dense annotation. Existing vision-language grounding methods typically require report-derived findings at inference, making localization dependent on paired text and limiting each forward pass to a queried finding. We propose Instance-Guided Report Anchoring (IGRA), a model-agnostic module that preserves the correspondence between each annotated abnormality instance and the report finding that describes it. IGRA pools each instance representation and anchors it to the corresponding finding embedding during training; all text-related components are discarded at inference. We further reformulate free-text grounding on ReXGroundingCT as multi-label volumetric segmentation by merging same-category instances, allowing all abnormality categories to be predicted in one image-only forward pass. IGRA improves Dice by 22.5% over the strongest image-only baseline (30.93 vs. 25.25) and is comparable to VoxTell on the single-finding subset (30.29 vs. 30.43). Applied unchanged to four standard 3D segmentation backbones, IGRA improves Dice and hit rate across all architectures. Zero-shot evaluation on LIDC-IDRI, PleThora, and a private in-house dataset further shows consistent gains over image-only baselines.

</details>


### [20] [SAM3-LoRA: Parameter-Efficient Adaptation of a Concept-Promptable Foundation Model for Multi-Class Structural Defect Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00469)
*P. Malaisree,S. Youwai,S. Janrungautai,D. Amorndechaphon,P. Rojanavasu,W. Songkitti*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究将低秩适配（LoRA）应用于SAM3模型以进行多类别结构缺陷分割，提出了一种直接从COCO式类别标注监督概念可提示模型的方法，并识别出正样本仅提示导致的"存在性预测解耦"失效模式，通过穷举硬负样本提示加以缓解，在两个数据集上实现了显著的分割性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAM等提示式分割基础模型虽然支持开放词汇概念并通过文本提示返回所有匹配实例，但将其全量微调适配到特定专业领域在计算上对中小组织而言成本过高。作者旨在探索一种计算高效的适配方式，使基础分割模型能够有效服务于结构缺陷检测等专业下游任务，同时无需依赖人工设计的提示模板或学习型类别嵌入。

Method: 本研究采用LoRA对SAM3进行参数高效微调，适配多类别结构缺陷分割任务。方法包含两个核心贡献：（1）提出一种监督过程，直接用类别名称作为提示，从COCO式类别标注的实例分割数据训练概念可提示模型，无需提示模板、同义词扩展或学习型类别嵌入；（2）识别并缓解了一项特定失效模式——由于常规标注文件中仅包含正样本提示，模型的存在性预测与文本条件解耦，退化为对任意提示都响应（这种坍塌在仅基于正样本提示的评估指标中不可见），通过在图像中缺失的每个数据集类别发出零检测查询（穷举硬负样本提示）来解决该问题，且不增加标注成本。同时对比了两种适配器放置方式，分别更新0.121%和1.341%的模型参数。

Result: 在自建的隧道衬砌数据集上，像素IoU从0.017提升至0.338，实例级召回率从0.375提升至0.672；在独立的公开Structural Defects Dataset上，从0.017提升至0.855，召回率从0.574提升至1.000。两个数据集的十项评估指标改进方向一致，且最大的逐类别增益恰好出现在零样本能力缺失的类别上。

Conclusion: LoRA可使SAM3高效适配专业结构缺陷分割任务，少量参数的更新即可取得显著性能提升。该研究揭示了正样本独有标注下概念提示模型特有的退化问题，并提出了一种零标注成本的硬负样本提示策略加以克服，验证了参数高效适配方案在跨数据集上的泛化能力。

Abstract: Promptable segmentation foundation models such as SAM3 accept an open-vocabulary text concept and return every instance matching it, but adapting them to a specialized domain by full fine-tuning is computationally prohibitive for the organizations that would benefit most. This study applies Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) to SAM3 for multi-class structural defect segmentation and examines both how such a model can be supervised from conventional annotation and whether the resulting efficiency gain transfers across datasets. Two contributions are methodological. First, we describe a supervision procedure that trains a concept-promptable model directly from COCO-style class-labeled instance segmentation by using the category name itself as the prompt, requiring no prompt templates, no synonym expansion, and no learned class embeddings. Second, we identify and mitigate a failure mode specific to this setting: because a conventional annotation file yields positive prompts exclusively, the model's presence prediction decouples from the text condition and degenerates into responding to any prompt, a collapse that is invisible to every metric computed on positive prompts alone. Exhaustive hard-negative prompting, in which every dataset category absent from an image is issued as a zero-detection query, addresses this at no annotation cost. Two adapter placements were compared under an identical protocol, updating 0.121% and 1.341% of model parameters. On a purpose-built tunnel lining dataset, pixel intersection-over-union improved from 0.017 to 0.338 and instance-level recall from 0.375 to 0.672; on the independent public Structural Defects Dataset, from 0.017 to 0.855 and from 0.574 to 1.000. Improvements were directionally consistent across ten metrics on both datasets, and the largest per-category gains occurred precisely where zero-shot competence was absent.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出一个自动管线，通过显式运用正负空间进行视觉概念混合，结合视觉-语言模型的语义推理、几何约束、混合像素-矢量管线以及多模态智能体，实现了更具表现力和可识别性的概念融合图像生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图形设计师常通过融合视觉概念并在单一图像中利用正负空间来传达多重含义，但现有的自动概念混合计算方法大多忽视了空间构图（即正负空间运用）在其中的关键作用。

Method: 方法包含四个关键组件：(1) 结合视觉-语言模型的语义推理与真实示例的几何约束来确定概念整合的可行区域；(2) 采用混合像素-矢量管线——先以扩散模型inpainting生成快速粗略初始结果，再通过点级矢量优化进行精修以确保结构一致性和语义平衡表达；(3) 多模态智能体作为规划器和评估器协调整个过程，支持迭代改进和可解释控制。

Result: 通过基线对比和用户研究评估，该方法在表现力、创造力和概念可识别性方面均优于现有方法，能有效利用正负空间提升生成质量；并在可控图像生成和信息图生成等多种应用中展示了良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该研究填补了自动概念混合中空间构图缺失的空白，证明显式建模正负空间可以显著提升概念混合的生成质量，且该管线具有跨应用场景的广泛适用性。

Abstract: Graphic designers often blend visual concepts to communicate multiple ideas within a single image, leveraging positive and negative space to create balance, emphasis, and aesthetic appeal. While computational methods have begun to support automatic concept blending, they largely overlook the role of spatial composition in the design. To address this gap, we present an automatic pipeline that explicitly applies positive and negative space throughout the blending process. Our approach first identifies plausible regions for concept integration by combining semantic reasoning from vision-language models with geometric constraints derived from real-world examples. Conditioned on these regions, the system generates blended compositions using a hybrid pixel-vector pipeline: diffusion-based inpainting produces a fast, coarse initialization, which is then refined through vector-based optimization at the point level to ensure structural coherence and balanced semantic expression. A multimodal agent orchestrates this process as a planner and evaluator, enabling iterative improvement and interpretable control. Through an evaluation using both baseline comparisons and a user study, we demonstrate greater expressiveness, creativity, and concept recognizability by effectively leveraging positive and negative space. We further demonstrate the generalizability of our approach across diverse applications, including controllable image and infographic generation.

</details>


### [22] [ViTAL-X: Video-Text Alignment with Cross-Modal Temporal Edits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00505)
*Sethuraman T,Savya Khosla,Onkar Kishor Susladkar,Aditi Tiwari,Seoung Wug Oh,Kushal Kafle,Joon-Young Lee,Derek Hoiem,Simon Jenni*
