Method: RESELF是一个统一框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 将预训练于大规模离体(exocentric)数据上的几何基础模型适配到自我中心视频，通过帧级尺度和相对位姿一致性目标实现确定性度量几何重建；(2) 利用相机轨迹和潜在几何特征作为条件，通过扩散模型生成穿戴者的全身运动；(3) 通过闭环运动学反馈阶段在保持场景几何的同时细化相机头部位姿。此外，作者从EgoExo4D数据集中整理出EE4D-JSM数据集，将自我中心视频、稀疏度量场景几何、相机轨迹和全身运动标注对齐。

Result: 在深度估计、相机跟踪和全身运动估计三个任务上，RESELF均超越了针对各单独任务设计的最先进方法。

Conclusion: RESELF证明将确定性几何重建与生成式运动推理耦合的统一框架能够有效解决自我中心视频中场景与穿戴者联合恢复的挑战性任务，并将在后续公开代码、模型和数据集。

Abstract: Complete 3D perception from egocentric video requires recovering the surrounding scene and the wearer's full-body motion in a shared metric frame. Existing methods typically address scene reconstruction and motion estimation separately: scene reconstruction methods ignore the wearer, whereas motion estimation methods lack explicit scene geometry and often depend on external trajectories. Joint recovery is challenging because the two tasks exhibit asymmetric visibility and require different prediction paradigms. The largely visible scene supports deterministic geometric regression, whereas the severely occluded body requires generative motion inference. We therefore propose RESELF (REconstructing the Scene and the sELF), a unified framework that couples deterministic metric geometry reconstruction with geometry-conditioned motion generation. RESELF adapts a geometry foundation model pre-trained on large-scale exocentric data to egocentric video using frame-wise scale and relative-pose consistency objectives. The resulting camera trajectory and latent geometric features condition a diffusion model that recovers the wearer's motion. A subsequent closed-loop kinematic feedback stage further refines the camera head while preserving the reconstructed scene geometry. To support training and evaluation, we curate EE4D-JSM from EgoExo4D by aligning egocentric video, sparse metric scene geometry, camera trajectories, and full-body motion annotations. Experiments show that RESELF outperforms state-of-the-art methods designed for the individual tasks across depth estimation, camera tracking, and full-body motion estimation. Code, models, and datasets will be available at https://ka1guan.github.io/RESELF/.

</details>


### [90] [HiLRP: Toward One Trustworthy Explanation for Vision Transformer: Conservation-Valid Attribution via Attention Primitives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01282)
*Sathiyamohan Nishankar,Pubudu Sanjeewani,Asanka Perera,Selvarajah Thuseethan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HiLRP提出了一种统一的、基于层间相关传播（LRP）的可解释性归因框架，将当前ViT架构中的注意力与分辨率缩减操作分解为四种基本算子类型（线性映射、双线性混合、归一化/门控、重索引），每种算子都有满足守恒性质的相关性规则，从而无需针对每个架构单独推导即可支持新的ViT骨干网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ViT骨干网络架构日益多样化（卷积stem、窗口/线性/多轴注意力、patch merging、空间缩减等），现有归因方法（如Grad-CAM、attention rollout、LRP）各自依赖特定架构假设，无法在ViT变体中保持可靠。目前缺乏一个统一的、跨越整个架构空间的归因框架。

Method: 将各类ViT中的注意力算子和分辨率缩减算子解构为四种基本运算类型：线性映射、双线性混合、归一化或门控、重索引。每种运算推导出满足守恒性质的相关性传播规则。使用这些规则组合构成HiLRP归因框架，使新骨干网络能够通过构造而非逐架构推导获得支持。

Result: 验证了守恒性和条件等变性到机器精度。在14种归因方法和10种架构的对比中，所有先前方法在ViT家族中均不可靠；对空间掩码鲁棒的骨干中，Faithfulness Correlation变得无信息。HiLRP是唯一在windowed、空间缩减、多轴和线性注意力模型中保持守恒性质的方法（朴素扩展会产生零或膨胀的相关性）。在EfficientViT上的类激活映射归因失败定位中，HiLRP达到0.97的Pointing得分，远优于竞争方法的0.55。

Conclusion: HiLRP通过统一的四种算子解构，提供了一种可证明满足守恒性质、能跨ViT架构家族通用的归因方法，避免了针对每个新架构的特定推导，并在归因可靠性上显著优于现有的14种对比方法。

Abstract: Vision Transformer (ViT) design has become increasingly diverse, with backbones combining convolutional stems, windowed, linear, or multi-axis attention, patch merging, and spatial reduction in various configurations. This diversity poses challenges for existing attribution methods, whose assumptions often do not hold across ViT variants: Grad-CAM requires a terminal spatial feature map, attention rollout assumes global softmax attention, and layer-wise relevance propagation (LRP) requires module-specific rules. To the best of our knowledge, no existing method provides a unified attribution framework across this architectural space. We show that this architectural diversity can be captured by a simpler underlying structure. The attention and resolution-reduction operators in current ViTs can be decomposed into four operation types: linear maps, bilinear mixing, normalization or gating, and reindexing. Each operation admits a relevance rule that satisfies conservation. Based on these rules, HiLRP supports new backbones by construction rather than by architecture-specific derivation, and its attribution maps decompose the prediction rather than relying on heuristic assumptions. We prove conservation and conditional equivariance and verify both to machine precision. Across 14 attribution methods and 10 architectures, we find that no prior method remains reliable across ViT families, while Faithfulness Correlation becomes uninformative for backbones robust to spatial masking. HiLRP alone preserves conservation across windowed, spatial-reduction, multi-axis, and linear-attention models, where naive extensions can produce zero or inflated relevance. It also localizes attribution failures in class activation mapping, achieving 0.97 Pointing compared with 0.55 for competing methods on EfficientViT.

</details>


### [91] [Agentic Multimodal Models for Environmental Hyperspectral Unmixing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01289)
*Michał Cholewa,Luca Ciampi,Nicola Messina,Przemysław Głomb,Giuseppe Amato*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种算法无关的大视觉语言模型（LVLM）驱动的智能体框架，用于细化而非替换现有的高光谱解混模块化管道。通过迭代收集光谱和空间证据（如光谱库检索、丰度图可视化），并进行端元合并/删除操作与丰度重估，该智能体在多个数据集上一致提升了端元基数、光谱特征和丰度图质量，且与端到端解混方法（如CNN-AE、uDAS、R-CoNMF）具有竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统模块化高光谱解混方法按顺序执行模型阶数估计、端元提取和丰度估计，各阶段的误差累积会导致冗余或模糊的候选组分，最终影响分解质量。现有方法缺乏对异构管道输出的系统化精修机制，因此需要一种不依赖特定底层算法、能够整合光谱与空间证据的通用细化框架。

Method: 提出一个LVLM驱动的智能体框架：以初始分解为起点，智能体通过专用工具（光谱库检索、丰度图可视化等）迭代收集互补的光谱与空间证据，并通过合并（merge）和删除（discard）操作修改活动端元集合，随后进行丰度重估。该精修过程被应用于多种结合不同模型阶数估计、端元提取和丰度估计方法的模块化管道中，算法无关地适用于不同底层管道。

Result: 在HYDICE Urban、Jasper Ridge和Stonewall Playa三个数据集上的实验表明，所提智能体在多种异构模块化管道的端元基数上一致提升，在恢复的光谱特征和丰度图方面总体优于基线，并与集成式端到端解混方法（CNN-AE、uDAS、R-CoNMF）相比保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 工具使用的LVLM智能体能够有效结合光谱与空间证据，对物理可解释的高光谱解混分解进行算法无关的精修。该研究展示了大型视觉语言模型智能体在高光谱解混等物理量测任务中作为后处理细化器的潜力，代码已公开。

Abstract: Hyperspectral unmixing is a key task in remote sensing that aims to decompose mixed pixels in hyperspectral images into their constituent material signatures, or endmembers, and their fractional abundances. Conventional modular approaches estimate the scene composition through successive model-order estimation, endmember extraction, and abundance estimation stages, whose errors can lead to redundant or ambiguous candidate components and ultimately affect the recovered decomposition. We introduce an algorithm-agnostic, large vision-language model (LVLM)-driven agentic framework that refines the outputs of such pipelines rather than replacing their underlying numerical algorithms. Starting from an initial decomposition, the agent iteratively gathers complementary spectral and spatial evidence through dedicated tools, including spectral-library retrieval and abundance-map visualization, and modifies the active endmember set through merge and discard operations followed by abundance re-estimation. We apply the same refinement procedure to several modular pipelines combining different model-order, extraction, and abundance-estimation methods, and evaluate it on HYDICE Urban, Jasper Ridge, and Stonewall Playa. Experiments show that the proposed agent consistently improves endmember cardinality and generally improves the recovered spectral signatures and abundance maps across heterogeneous modular pipelines, while remaining competitive with integrated end-to-end unmixing methods, including CNN-AE, uDAS, and R-CoNMF. These results highlight the potential of tool-using LVLM agents to combine spectral and spatial evidence for algorithm-agnostic refinement of physically grounded hyperspectral unmixing decompositions. Code is publicly available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/agentic-hu.

</details>


### [92] [CMRVision: A Foundation Model for Cardiac MR Image Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01308)
*Athira J. Jacob,Puneet Sharma,Daniel Rueckert*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了CMRVision，一个基于DINOv3样式自监督学习、在36百万张多中心多序列CMR图像上训练的心脏磁共振成像（CMR）专用基础模型。系统评估了架构和训练设计选择（如patch size、patch级目标），在两个下游任务（多任务分割和cine视图分类）上验证，结果显示CMR专用预训练、更小patch size和patch级目标能一致提升性能，在分割和分类任务上均超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CMR成像在多个序列和视图中提供互补的心脏解剖、功能和组织特征信息，但目前缺乏针对CMR领域定制的专用基础模型。现有的自然图像或通用医学图像基础模型未能充分利用CMR多序列、多视图的特有特征，因此需要探索CMR特定领域的预训练方法和设计选择。

Method: 使用DINOv3风格的自监督学习在36百万张多中心多序列CMR图像上预训练CMR专用基础模型CMRVision。系统评估了不同架构和训练设计选择（如patch size、预训练数据源、目标函数），随后在两个下游任务中评估：覆盖cine、LGE和mapping序列的多任务分割，以及cine视图分类。还包括在未见过的LGE长轴视图上的零样本分割测试。

Result: CMRVision在分割基准上取得最强整体性能，超越了自然图像、医学图像、监督学习及CMR基础模型基线。Dice分数：LV为0.940-0.967，心肌为0.855-0.905，RV为0.929，LA为0.920，RA为0.931。在LGE和mapping图像的心肌分割上提升最大。零样本LGE长轴分割平均Dice为0.692。视图分类平均准确率达0.906，优于文献中已有方法。

Conclusion: CMR专用预训练、更小的patch size以及patch级目标函数能一致提升CMR下游任务性能。CMRVision在跨序列和跨视图的心脏MRI分析中展现出稳健性和泛化能力，具备作为CMR分析通用基础模型的潜力。

Abstract: Cardiac magnetic resonance (CMR) imaging provides complementary information on cardiac anatomy, function, and tissue characterization across multiple sequences and views. In this work, we investigate foundation model pretraining for 2D CMR and introduce CMRVision, a CMR-specific foundation model trained using DINOv3-style self-supervised learning on a multi-center, multi-sequence cohort of 36 million CMR images. We systematically evaluate architectural and training design choices for domain-specific pretraining. CMRVision is evaluated on two downstream tasks: multi-task segmentation across cine, late gadolinium enhancement (LGE), and mapping sequences, and cine view classification. Our experiments show that CMR-specific pretraining, smaller patch sizes, and patch-level objectives consistently improve downstream performance. Across a multi-task segmentation benchmark, CMRVision achieved the strongest overall performance, outperforming prior natural-image (NI), medical-image, supervised, and CMR foundation model baselines. Improvements were modest but consistent across structures and sequences, with Dice scores ranging from 0.940-0.967 for LV and 0.855-0.905 for myocardium, and reaching 0.929 for RV, 0.920 for LA, and 0.931 for RA. The largest gains were observed for myocardium segmentation in LGE and mapping images. In a zero-shot segmentation task on unseen LGE long-axis views, the model achieved an average Dice score of 0.692, demonstrating cross-view generalization. For cine view classification, CMRVision achieved the highest average accuracy (0.906), compared to prior methods reported in the literature. These results highlight the potential of CMRVision to support robust and generalizable cardiac MRI analysis across multiple sequences and views.

</details>


### [93] [Reliability Challenges in Diffusion Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01318)
*Md. Atabuzzaman,Chris Thomas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次系统评估了扩散式大型视觉语言模型（dLVLMs）在幻觉和偏见方面的可靠性，发现其逆转了自回归模型的yes-bias、在少数种族群体上准确率趋近于零、存在与去噪步数相关的长度先验及机械性幻觉信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散式大型视觉语言模型（dLVLMs）虽然在并行解码、双向上下文和可控生成方面具有优势，但其可靠性属性（幻觉和偏见）尚未被系统性地刻画，缺少全面的基准评估。

Method: 对六个扩散式LVLM模型与竞争性自回归（AR）基线模型进行基准对比，从四个维度系统评估幻觉（hallucination）和偏见（bias）表现，并分析去噪过程中的机制信号。

Result: 研究发现：(1) dLVLMs逆转了AR模型在二元视觉查询中的yes-bias；(2) 幻觉率相当但语言质量下降；(3) 在少数种族群体上准确率骤降至接近零，且存在极性相反的不平等性别偏见；(4) 在多项选择场景中，当正确答案比干扰项更短时会出现准确率崩塌，这与首个去噪步骤即出现的长度先验有关。晚期低置信度去噪步骤提交的token与幻觉内容相关。

Conclusion: 不同模型家族的可靠性模式存在差异，说明可靠性由生成范式与训练数据共同塑造，扩散生成机制中存在独特的可解释性信号，为理解和改进dLVLMs的可靠性提供了方向。

Abstract: Diffusion-based Large Vision-Language Models (dLVLMs) have recently emerged as a compelling alternative to autoregressive (AR) LVLMs, offering advantages in parallel decoding, bidirectional context, and controllable generation. Despite rapid progress, their reliability properties remain largely uncharacterized. We present the first systematic reliability evaluation of hallucination and bias in dLVLMs, benchmarking six diffusion models against competitive AR baselines across four dimensions. Our key findings are: (1) dLVLMs reverse the yes-bias of AR models in binary visual queries; (2) they achieve competitive hallucination rates yet exhibit degraded linguistic quality; (3) they collapse to near-zero accuracy on underrepresented racial groups with opposite-polarity gender bias; and (4) they exhibit accuracy collapse in multiple-choice settings when the correct option is shorter than its distractors, associated with a length prior that emerges at the first denoising step. Tokens committed at late denoising steps with low confidence further correlate with hallucinated content, pointing to a mechanistic signal unique to diffusion generation. These patterns vary across model families, suggesting reliability is shaped by the generative paradigm together with training data.

</details>


### [94] [ExBind: A Controlled Diagnostic Benchmark for Visual-to-Executable Correspondence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01344)
*Ziqian Wang,Yuxiao Cheng,Tingxiong Xiao,Jinli Suo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ExBind是一个用于诊断多模态编码/编辑系统中"视觉到可执行绑定"层错误的受控基准。它隔离语义定位与动作执行之间的对应环节，将潜在绑定实例编译为SVG、DOM、canvas、树、图和数据表等6类案例，要求模型输出严格引用而不依赖推理痕迹进行评估。基准包含250例广谱套件、240例目标套件和50个配对潜在组。实验显示Qwen2.5-VL-3B达到98.4%候选有效性但仅76.4%精确准确率，其残差错误表现为"正确行/错误列"的系统性模式；Qwen3-VL-4B达到100%有效性和98.8%精确准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态编码与编辑系统需要将可见或语义指代物映射到可编辑的确切可执行对象，但错误的引用可能选中有效但不正确的DOM节点、SVG元素等，单纯的执行成功无法揭示失败根源。现有方法缺少一个能够隔离和诊断"视觉—可执行对应"这一特定层的受控基准。

Method: ExBind将视觉到可执行的对应层抽取为一个受控诊断基准，介于语义定位与动作执行之间。它采样与表示无关的潜在绑定实例，并将其编译为SVG、DOM、canvas、树、图和数据表案例，提供到可执行引用的确定性映射。模型只输出严格引用，评估器将预测映射回潜在结构并按结构约束打分，无需推理痕迹。基准包含250例宽泛套件、240例互斥目标套件及50个配对潜在组，并设计了候选顺序扰动实验。

Result: Qwen2.5-VL-3B在候选有效性上达到98.4%，但精确准确率仅为76.4%；Qwen3-VL-4B则达到100.0%的有效性和98.8%的精确准确率。在针对性表格套件中，Qwen2.5-VL-3B的所有残差错误均为"正确的行但错误的列"选择。候选顺序扰动会改变个案级别的结果，但保持这一错误模式不变。

Conclusion: ExBind旨在进行受控诊断而非大规模排名或端到端编辑评估。实验揭示模型即使能产出有效候选引用，仍可能在精确的视觉到可执行绑定上失败，且错误具有系统性方向（如表场景中的行对列错误），表明需要针对绑定层进行专门优化。代码和基准数据已公开。

Abstract: Multimodal coding and editing systems must map a visible or semantic referent to the exact executable object that can be edited. A wrong reference may select a valid but incorrect DOM node, SVG element, graph endpoint, hierarchy member, or table cell, while final execution success alone does not reveal the source of the failure. ExBind isolates this visual-to-executable correspondence layer as a controlled diagnostic benchmark between semantic localization and action execution. It samples representation-independent latent binding instances and compiles them into SVG, DOM, canvas, tree, graph, and table cases with deterministic mappings to executable references. Models output only a strict reference; the evaluator maps predictions back to latent structure and scores structural constraints without requiring reasoning traces. The release contains a 250-case broad suite, a disjoint 240-case targeted suite, and 50 paired latent groups. Qwen2.5-VL-3B achieves 98.4% candidate validity but 76.4% exact accuracy, while Qwen3-VL-4B achieves 100.0% validity and 98.8% exact accuracy. In the targeted table suite, all Qwen2.5-VL-3B residual errors are valid correct-row/wrong-column selections. Candidate-order perturbations change case-level outcomes while preserving this error pattern. ExBind is designed for controlled diagnosis rather than population-scale ranking or end-to-end editing evaluation. Code and benchmark records are available at https://github.com/Daerwang2020/Exbind and https://huggingface.co/datasets/Ziqianwwww/ExBind.

</details>


### [95] [Accurate Reconstruction of Gas Turbine Blade Geometry Using 3D/2D Rigid Registration and CT View Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01368)
*Hristo Valtchanov,Nicolas Piché,Vladimir Brailovski,Justin Byers,Catherine Désrosiers,François Guibault*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多部分3D-2D刚性配准方法，将CAD模型与X射线投影图像对齐作为CT重建的替代方案，用于燃气轮机叶片等复杂内部结构部件的无损检测与测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 燃气轮机叶片冷却通道等复杂内部结构的尺寸精度直接影响热性能和使用寿命，传统的CT重建检测成本高、流程复杂。需要一种更高效的替代方法来对齐CAD模型与X射线投影，实现部件检测和测量。

Method: 采用分步配准策略：(1) 通过贪心配准算法依次对齐叶片外部轮廓，再配准内部组件；(2) 以模拟X射线图像与实际采集图像之间的互信息最大化为目标；(3) 使用贪心方法迭代优化视角角度，以最小化尺寸测量误差。

Result: 少量斜向视角即可获得最佳精度，较宽范围的视角也能产生可接受的结果。方法实现亚像素配准精度，误差小于放大后探测器像素间距的五分之一。图像噪声和缺陷会降低配准精度，但与CT重建相比，在投影空间中直接配准可减轻这些不利影响。

Conclusion: 通过合理的视角选择，即使在存在图像噪声和缺陷的情况下，该方法仍能保持可接受的亚像素配准精度，为复杂内部结构部件的无损检测提供了一种比CT重建更高效且鲁棒的替代方案。

Abstract: Non-destructive X-ray and computed tomography (CT) testing are essential for ensuring the dimensional accuracy of manufactured components with complex internal structures, such as the cooling channels in gas turbine blades, which directly affect thermal performance and service life. This study presents a multipart 3D-2D rigid registration approach for aligning CAD models with X-ray projections as an alternative to CT reconstruction for part inspection and measurement. A greedy registration algorithm sequentially aligns the blade's exterior before registering its internal components by maximizing the mutual information between simulated and acquired X-ray images. This stepwise approach reduces problem complexity and improves alignment accuracy. View angles are optimized using a greedy method that iteratively selects angles to minimize dimensional measurement errors. The results indicate that a small number of oblique views provides the best accuracy, although a broad range of angles yields acceptable results. The method achieves subpixel registration accuracy, with errors below one-fifth of the magnified detector-pixel pitch. Image noise and defects reduce registration precision, but direct registration in projection space mitigates these effects compared with CT reconstruction. Appropriate view selection can therefore preserve acceptable subpixel accuracy in the presence of image noise and defects.

</details>


### [96] [Diffusion Based Unpaired Data Learning for Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01370)
*Chenglong Bao,Yiming Dang,Chenguang Duan,Yuling Jiao,Defeng Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LUD-DIF，一种基于扩散模型的非配对数据逆向问题求解方法。该方法从联合分布的证据下界出发，在弱耦合假设下将其解耦为两个独立的扩散过程，从变分推断角度提供理论支撑，推导损失函数，定量分析假设引入的误差界，并提出超参数选择的理论启发式规则。实验证明其在多个图像逆向问题上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多基于深度学习的逆向问题求解器依赖大量配对数据，但在实际场景中获取充足配对数据极为困难，而非配对数据成本低廉。为最大化数据利用率，需要在仅有非配对数据的条件下实现高效的逆向问题求解。

Method: 提出LUD-DIF方法：从联合分布的证据下界（ELBO）出发，在弱耦合假设下将ELBO解耦为两个独立的扩散过程；从变分推断视角提供理论依据并推导损失函数；定量分析弱耦合假设引入的误差上界；基于定理给出超参数选择的理论启发式规则。

Result: 在多个图像逆向问题上，LUD-DIF取得了优异的性能，验证了其在非配对数据逆向问题设置下的有效性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: LUD-DIF通过在非配对数据下解耦扩散过程并配合坚实的理论保障，有效解决了配对数据不足的限制，在图像逆向问题上表现卓越，为该类问题提供了可行的范式。

Abstract: Data is important in many deep learning-based inverse problem solvers. However, obtaining sufficient paired data in many scenarios remains highly challenging, while unpaired data is cheap. To maximize data utilization, this paper proposes LUD-DIF, a diffusion-based approach for solving inverse problems with unpaired data. Starting from the evidence lower bound (ELBO) of the joint distribution, we decouple it into two independent diffusion processes under the weak-coupling assumption. The method provides theoretical support from a variational inference perspective, derives the loss function, quantitatively analyzes the error bound introduced by the assumption, and offers a theorem-motivated heuristic for hyperparameter selection. Experimental results demonstrate that LUD-DIF achieves outstanding performance on multiple image inverse problems, validating its effectiveness and generalization capability in unpaired inverse problem settings.

</details>


### [97] [IntroConformal: Conformal Factuality Guarantees for Large Vision-Language Models via Introspective Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01375)
*Md. Atabuzzaman,Christian Alexander,Chris Thomas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出IntroConformal，一个基于模型内在信号（introspective signals）的免训练Conformal Risk Control（CRC）框架，为大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）提供有限样本、分布无关的事实性统计保证，无需外部验证器或生成时的置信度信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LVLMs在生成内容上缺乏事实性保证。现有提供统计保证的方法依赖外部验证器或生成时置信度信号，前者引入额外依赖，后者常对"自信但错误"的输出失效。本文主张可以通过模型自身的自省信号实现可靠的事实性控制。

Method: 提出IntroConformal框架：(1) 基于CRC的免训练框架，提供有限样本、分布无关的事实性保证；(2) 提出层间语义稳定性（layer-wise semantic stability）作为基于隐藏状态的conformity score；(3) 进一步提出验证概率（verification probability），一种更强的score，捕获模型对主张事实性的自我管理判断。

Result: 在多种LVLM架构上，IntroConformal满足conformal risk保证，同时大幅减少abstention（弃权），并在claim-level判别能力上达到与基于外部验证器baseline相当或更优的性能。

Conclusion: 仅通过模型自身的自省信号即可实现与外部验证器相当甚至更优的事实性控制，同时避免额外依赖，验证了自省式事实性控制范式的有效性。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) have achieved strong multimodal performance, yet ensuring the factual correctness of generated content remains challenging. Existing methods that provide statistical guarantees on factuality typically rely on external verifiers or generation-time confidence signals, which introduce auxiliary dependencies or often fail for confident but incorrect outputs. We argue that reliable factuality control can instead be achieved through introspective signals derived from the model itself. We introduce IntroConformal, a training-free Conformal Risk Control (CRC) framework that provides finite-sample, distribution-free factuality guarantees. We first instantiate it with layer-wise semantic stability, a conformity score derived from hidden-state representations, and then propose verification probability, a stronger score capturing the model's self-administered judgment on claim factuality. Across multiple LVLM architectures, IntroConformal satisfies the conformal risk guarantee while substantially reducing abstention and achieving competitive or superior claim-level discrimination relative to external verifier-based baselines.

</details>


### [98] [Multimodal RGB-Infrared Combination for UAV-Based Wildfire Segmentation: A Comparative Study on FLAME3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01390)
*Matheus F. Kovaleski,Luís Garrote,Cristiano Premebida,Jérôme Mendes,João Ruivo Paulo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了UAV森林火灾分割中RGB与红外图像的融合策略，在FLAME3数据集上比较了U-Net、DeepLabV3+和SegFormer三种架构下三种代表性融合方法，发现热红外信息占主导地位，特征级多模态融合结合Transformer架构是未来最有前景的方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UAV因灵活性、低成本和高分辨率成像能力成为火灾监测的重要平台，深度学习进一步提升了其能力。本文旨在系统分析RGB与红外多模态融合中每个模态的贡献、融合时机的影响，以及不同网络架构如何利用多模态信息进行UAV野火分割。

Method: 在FLAME3数据集上，研究者对比了RGB和红外单模态基线，以及三种代表性融合策略（推测涵盖输入级、特征级等不同融合时机），并在三种分割架构（U-Net、DeepLabV3+、SegFormer）上进行系统性评估，以实现二值野火分割任务。

Result: 研究发现热红外信息在UAV野火分割中起主导作用；特征级多模态融合结合基于Transformer的架构（如SegFormer）表现最佳，是最有前景的研究方向。

Conclusion: 该工作为UAV野火监测中的多模态融合设计提供了实证指导：红外模态不可或缺，融合时机应侧重特征级，架构选择上Transformer类模型更能充分利用多模态互补信息，为后续研究指明了方向。

Abstract: Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) have emerged as a promising platform for firefighting operations due to their flexibility, low operational cost, and ability to acquire high-resolution imagery in locations that may be difficult or dangerous to access using conventional methods. Recent advances in deep learning have significantly improved the capabilities of UAV-based wildfire monitoring systems. The present work investigates RGB-infrared fusion for binary wildfire segmentation on the FLAME3 dataset. In this Study, RGB and Infrared baselines are compared with three representative fusion strategies across three segmentation architectures, including U-Net, DeepLabV3+, and SegFormer. The key motivation of this work is to analyze the contribution of each modality, evaluate the impact of fusion timing, and examine how different network architectures exploit multimodal information for UAV wildfire delineation. The findings indicate that thermal information plays a dominant role in UAV segmentation and that feature-level multimodal fusion combined with transformer-based architectures offers the most promising direction for future research.

</details>


### [99] [Scale-based Approach for Active Wildfire Segmentation on Satellite Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01392)
*Matheus F. Kovaleski,Cristiano Premebida,João Ruivo Paulo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于数据驱动的火区尺寸分布特征化协议，用于评估多尺度野火条件下Landsat-8多光谱影像的主动火分割性能。比较了U-Net、DeepLabV3+和SegFormer三种架构在不同SWIR光谱配置下的表现，发现U-Net鲁棒性最强，SWIR2波段对主动火分割最为关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卫星影像中的主动火（active fire）像素稀疏且高度不平衡，尤其在早期阶段或低密度火观测条件下，使得主动火测绘极具挑战性。现有研究缺乏对多尺度野火尺寸条件下模型鲁棒性的系统评估方法，需要一种数据驱动的协议来刻画火区尺寸分布并评价模型在不同局部火区密度下的表现。

Method: 提出一种数据驱动的协议：通过连通组件分析（connected-component analysis）和四分位距（interquartile range）准则来表征火区尺寸分布。在此协议下，评估三种分割架构——U-Net、DeepLabV3+和SegFormer——在不同SWIR光谱配置下的主动火分割性能，并考察模型在低主动火像素密度图像上的鲁棒性。

Result: U-Net在各项评估条件下表现最强、鲁棒性最佳；SegFormer具有竞争力的性能；DeepLabV3+倾向于产生保守预测，召回率较低。跨架构比较中，SWIR2波段一致取得最强或接近最优的结果，凸显其在Landsat-8主动火分割中的重要性。

Conclusion: 光谱波段选择与架构设计对基于卫星的主动野火测绘（尤其在低主动火像素密度图像上训练）均至关重要。U-Net与SWIR2波段的组合是所评估条件下最具鲁棒性的选择。

Abstract: Active wildfire mapping from satellite imagery is challenging due to the sparse and highly imbalanced nature of fire pixels, especially in early-stage or low-density fire observations. This work investigates the use of multispectral Landsat-8 imagery for active-fire segmentation under multi-scale wildfire size conditions. We propose a data-driven protocol to characterize fire-region size distributions through connected-component analysis and an interquartile range criterion, enabling the evaluation of model robustness across different local fire-region densities. Three segmentation architectures, U-Net, DeepLabV3+, and SegFormer, are evaluated under different SWIR-based spectral configurations. Results show that U-Net achieves the strongest robustness across the evaluated conditions, SegFormer provides competitive performance, and DeepLabV3+ tends to produce conservative predictions with reduced recall. Across architectures, SWIR2 consistently achieves the strongest or near-best results, highlighting its importance for active-fire segmentation in Landsat-8 imagery. These findings suggest that both spectral band selection and architectural design are critical for robust satellite-based active wildfire mapping trained on low active fire-pixel density images.

</details>


### [100] [MegaStyle++: Scaling Image Style Space through Hierarchical Style Definition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01423)
*Junyao Gao,Sibo Liu,Jiaxing Li,Yanan Sun,Weidong Zhang,Cairong Zhao,Jun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文探讨了"什么是图像风格"这一基础问题，提出了一种层级式风格定义框架（从整体风格身份到细粒度视觉属性），并基于该定义构建了包含150K整体风格身份、1M细粒度风格提示和8M风格化图像的大规模数据集MegaStyle++-8M。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像风格是一个高度抽象且由人类构建的概念，受多种视觉因素影响并与内容内在纠缠，但学界始终缺乏对其统一且明确的定义，这阻碍了风格研究的系统化推进。

Method: 首先讨论风格的本质问题并定义层级式风格框架（从整体风格身份到细粒度视觉属性），提供更具结构性、可迁移性和可解释性的风格表示；随后基于该定义改进MegaStyle的标注流程，构建大规模风格数据集MegaStyle++-8M。

Result: 广泛的分析表明，层级式风格定义在多样性和语义广度上大幅扩展了风格空间，并能精确捕捉参考图像的内在视觉风格。

Conclusion: MegaStyle++为研究和大规模建模多样图像风格提供了可扩展的基础，数据集和代码已公开。

Abstract: Image style is a highly abstract, human-constructed concept shaped by a range of visual factors and intrinsically entangled with content, yet a unified and explicit definition of image style remains lacking. In this work, we first discuss the fundamental question of what is style and then propose a hierarchical style definition that describes image style from an overall style identity to fine-grained visual attributes, providing a more structured, transferable, and interpretable style representation. Based on this definition, we refine the style annotation pipeline of MegaStyle and construct MegaStyle++-8M, a large-scale style dataset containing 150K overall style identities, 1M fine-grained style prompts, and 8M stylized images. Extensive analyses demonstrate that our hierarchical definition substantially expands the style space in both diversity and semantic breadth, while precisely capturing intrinsic visual style of reference images. The dataset and code will be updated at https://github.com/Tencent/MegaStyle, we hope MegaStyle++ provides a scalable foundation for studying and modeling diverse image styles.

</details>


### [101] [Semantic-Guided Multimodal Preprocessing for Vision Transformer-Based Clear Cell Renal Cell Carcinoma Grading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01426)
*Fatemeh Javadian,Zhu Chen,Zahra Aminparast,Johannes Stegmaier*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种语义引导的多模态预处理方法，将现有预训练模型的细胞核分类图与RGB组织病理图像融合到Vision Transformer（ViT）中进行透明细胞肾细胞癌（CCRCC）分级，实现了0.916的平衡准确率，显著优于RGB基线（0.707）和此前研究的max-voting聚合方法（0.427）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CCRCC分级方法要么直接分析patch级图像，要么仅聚焦于细胞核级别分类，缺乏将细粒度细胞核分析结果与最终肿瘤分级相衔接的桥梁。作者旨在利用现有（尽管不完美）的细胞核分类模型中蕴含的诊断信息来提升ViT的粗粒度分级能力。

Method: 提出了语义引导的多模态预处理框架：将预训练模型生成的细胞核分类图与RGB组织病理图像进行融合，采用分类图通道拼接（channel concatenation）和乘性调制（multiplicative modulation）两种方式，并配合优化叠加（optimized overlays）策略，在保留RGB纹理特征的同时引入细胞核分级信息，供ViT进行最终分级。

Result: 语义引导增强方法取得0.916平衡准确率，大幅优于RGB-only基线（0.707）和此前研究的max-voting聚合方法（0.427）。灵敏度分析表明，即使模拟当前最先进细胞核分类模型的错误率扰动，21个百分点的提升依然保持。各类别召回率一致（0.93, 0.91, 0.91），表明增益并未集中于多数类别。但需注意扰动针对的是ground-truth标签图而非实际模型的预测输出。

Conclusion: 预处理层面的多模态融合能够有效利用现有不完美细胞核分类器的诊断潜力，成功弥合了此前相互割裂的细粒度核级分析与粗粒度ViT-patch分类之间的鸿沟。但真实上游模型部署场景下的鲁棒性验证留待未来工作。

Abstract: Clear cell renal cell carcinoma (CCRCC) grading is essential for treatment planning, yet existing approaches either analyze patch-level images directly or focus solely on nuclei-level classification, without linking to final tumor grading. We propose a semantic-guided multimodal preprocessing method that integrates nuclei classification maps from existing pre-trained models with RGB histopathology images for Vision Transformer (ViT)-based CCRCC grading. Our approach employs classification map channel concatenation and multiplicative modulation, with optimized overlays to leverage nuclei grading information, while preserving RGB textural features. Evaluation of multiple preprocessing strategies demonstrates that semantic-guided enhancement achieves 0.916 balanced accuracy, outperforming RGB-only baseline (0.707) and max-voting aggregation from prior studies (0.427). Sensitivity analysis reveals that this 21 percentage point improvement over baseline persists even under simulated perturbation at rates matching current state-of-the-art nuclei classification model error thresholds, suggesting both effective semantic utilization and practical robustness. These findings show that preprocessing-based multimodal fusion can leverage the diagnostic potential of existing imperfect nuclei classifiers, effectively bridging previously isolated fine-grained nuclear-level analysis with coarse-grained ViT-based patch classification. Per-class recall was consistent across grades (0.93, 0.91, 0.91), indicating that gains are not concentrated in the majority class. Because the sensitivity analysis perturbs ground-truth maps rather than predictions from an actual nuclei model, this result characterizes robustness under simulated error rather than deployment with a real upstream model, which remains for future work.

</details>


### [102] [Pix2Rep-v2: Data-Efficient Representation Learning for Dense Medical Imaging Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01427)
*S. Sifaoui,E. Angelini,S. Toupin,T. Pezel,L. Le Folgoc*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Pix2Rep-v2是一个用于医学图像像素级/体素级表示学习的密集自监督学习框架，通过像素级冗余减少目标和稠密表示等变性原则实现高效扩展至3D或宽视野应用。在四个数据集、多种任务和模态上验证了其少样本场景下的高数据效率，例如在M&Ms-2数据集的一次性分割任务中比全监督基线高出+9.3 Dice点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集自监督学习是无需标注即可学习局部描述符的强大范式，但要将其有效扩展到3D或宽视野医学成像任务并解决像素级表征学习的主要挑战仍是难点。现有方法在少样本下游应用中数据效率不高，需要一种能够高效扩展且适用于多种医学影像模态的密集SSL框架。

Method: Pix2Rep-v2采用像素级冗余减少目标（redundancy reduction objective），结合稠密表示的等变性（equivariance）原则进行密集自监督学习。同时提出一种基于稠密原型（dense prototype）的上下文变体（in-context variant），无需下游训练即可进行推理。框架可适配2D和3D架构，平均使用多骨干网络进行评估。

Result: 在四个数据集上跨多种任务、模态和解剖结构进行评估，覆盖2D和3D多骨干网络及多种数据规模。结果显示Pix2Rep-v2在少样本场景中相比全监督基线具有显著更高的数据效率，与当前最先进水平竞争力相当，在M&Ms-2数据集的一次性分割中达到+9.3 Dice点提升，代码与预训练模型已公开。

Conclusion: Pix2Rep-v2展示了密集自监督学习在医学影像少样本任务中的强大潜力，通过像素级冗余减少与等变性设计，结合无需训练的稠密原型上下文推理方案，在2D和3D多种医学影像场景下均实现了优越的数据效率，并开源了代码和预训练模型以促进社区应用。

Abstract: Dense self-supervised learning (SSL) is a powerful paradigm for learning without annotations the local descriptors required to solve dense medical imaging tasks. We present Pix2Rep-v2, a framework for SSL of pixel- and voxel-level representations suitable for few-shot downstream applications. Pix2Rep-v2 addresses the main challenges of dense SSL by leveraging a redundancy reduction objective at the pixel-level with a principle of equivariance of dense representations, that scales efficiently to 3D or wide field-of-view applications. We evaluate our method on four datasets, across multiple tasks, multiple modalities and anatomical structures using multiple backbones in 2D and 3D, and under various data regimes. As an alternative to linear probing or full fine-tuning on the downstream task, we also propose an in-context variant, without downstream training, based on a dense prototype approach. Pix2Rep-v2 shows substantially higher data-efficiency in few-shot scenarios compared to fully supervised baselines, and is competitive with the state-of-the-art e.g., +9.3 Dice points in one-shot segmentation on the M&Ms-2 dataset. Our code and pre-trained models are publicly available at https://github.com/BioMedTP/pix2rep-v2.

</details>


### [103] [Gaussian Core LoRA: Distribution-Aware Dynamic Adaptation for Broad Concept Erasure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01433)
*Qinghui Gong,Xunlei Chen,Yu-Xuan Zhang,Hua Meng,Zhengchun Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Gaussian Core LoRA，一种面向文本到图像扩散模型的概念擦除框架。它通过在高斯混合模型中拟合提示特征空间，识别目标概念内的潜在语义原型，并在推理时根据输入提示的高斯后验责任动态调节LoRA秩空间的残差重构，实现原型自适应的概念擦除。相比最强基线，ASR降低7.95%，COCO FID降低14.72%，CLIP Score提升4.98%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于适配器的方法（如LoRA）为每个目标概念分配静态的语义擦除方向，但一个广泛复杂的概念往往包含多个潜在语义原型（涉及不同物体、场景或关系），需要不同的局部擦除方向。单个LoRA更新平均了这些异构擦除需求，导致对困难原型欠擦除，同时对邻近良性语义过度编辑。

Method: 提出Gaussian Core LoRA框架：(1) 在提示特征空间中拟合高斯混合模型(GMM)以估计目标概念内的潜在语义原型；(2) 推理时将输入提示投影到特征空间，计算其高斯后验责任；(3) 后验责任条件化core generator，生成针对具体提示的、范数有界的LoRA秩空间残差重构，实现单一轻量适配器的原型自适应擦除。

Result: 相比各指标上最强基线：平均攻击成功率(ASR)降低7.95%，COCO Fréchet Inception Distance (FID)降低14.72%，CLIP Score提升4.98%。此外在对抗性提示鲁棒性、多身份/多风格擦除扩展性以及SDXL和FLUX架构兼容性上均表现良好。

Conclusion: Gaussian Core LoRA通过分布感知的低秩适配实现了原型级的概念擦除，克服了静态单一擦除方向的局限，在擦除效果、良性语义保留和部署效率之间取得了更优平衡。

Abstract: Concept erasure aims to suppress unsafe, privacy-sensitive, or undesirable generations in text-to-image diffusion models while preserving benign semantics, visual quality, and deployment efficiency. Existing adapter-based methods, such as Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA), typically freeze the diffusion backbone and learn lightweight parameter updates to steer generation away from target semantics. However, these methods usually assign a static semantic erasure direction to each target concept. This assumption is overly coarse for broad and complex target concepts, since a concept often contains multiple latent semantic prototypes involving different objects, scenes, or relations, and requires different local erasure directions. A single LoRA update averages these heterogeneous erasure demands, leading to under-erasure on difficult prototypes and over-editing of nearby benign semantics. To address this limitation, we propose Gaussian Core LoRA, a distribution-aware low-rank adaptation framework. It fits a Gaussian mixture model in the prompt feature space to estimate latent semantic prototypes within the target concept. During inference, each input prompt is projected into this feature space to compute its Gaussian posterior responsibilities, which condition the core generator to produce a prompt-specific, norm-bounded residual reconfiguration of the shared LoRA rank space. This enables prototype-adaptive erasure with a single lightweight adapter. Compared with the strongest baseline on each metric, Gaussian Core LoRA reduces average Attack Success Rate (ASR) by 7.95%, lowers COCO Fr'echet Inception Distance (FID) by 14.72%, and improves CLIP Score by 4.98%. Further experiments show robustness to adversarial prompts, scalability to multi-identity and multi-style erasure, and compatibility with SDXL and FLUX.

</details>


### [104] [RadMatch: Auditable Radiology Report Evaluation via Finding-Level Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01470)
*Charles Corbière,Léo Machado,Aubin Charley,Baptiste Callard,Pierre Manceron,Corentin Dancette*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RadMatch是一种多阶段、基于LLM的放射学报告评估指标，将报告比较分解为结构化的发现级匹配与显著性加权评分，并在七个临床属性维度上进行错误刻画。主评分为可操作错误数（可解释、可审计），同时提供分诊与安全性召回/精确率等补充视角。在两项专家基准上，RadMatch达到了与放射科医生间一致性相当或更优的水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM-based指标虽然与放射科医生判断相关性最高，但输出单一不透明分数，临床医生和模型构建者难以解释和审计其评估结果，缺乏可操作的反馈。

Method: RadMatch采用多阶段LLM评估管线：(1) 将报告比较分解为结构化finding级匹配；(2) 进行显著性加权评分；(3) 在七个临床属性维度（状态、位置、严重程度、形态、确定性、纵向比较、测量）上进行错误刻画；(4) 主评分为可操作错误数，候选finding被分为正确/部分/不正确，未匹配的计为遗漏或幻觉；(5) 提供分诊、可操作性安全性召回率/精确率及子集视图等部署导向指标。仅依赖few-shot prompting。

Result: 在两项专家基准上，RadMatch是最具临床对齐性的指标：在ReXVal上与放射科医生间一致性持平，在难度更高的RadEvalExpert上比此前最优指标提高一倍以上。

Conclusion: RadMatch通过提供可解释、可审计且临床对齐的结构化评估，弥补了现有黑箱式LLM指标的不足，并设计为可扩展到其他模态和解剖部位，将以开源代码和交互式仪表盘形式发布。

Abstract: As AI systems are increasingly used to draft radiology reports, reliably evaluating their clinical quality remains a critical challenge. Large language model (LLM)-based metrics are now the best-correlated with radiologist judgment, yet they output a single opaque score that neither a clinician nor a model builder can easily interpret or audit. We introduce RadMatch, a multi-stage, LLM-based metric that decomposes report comparison into a structured finding-level matching with significance-aware scoring and error characterization across seven clinical attribute dimensions (status, location, severity, morphology, certainty, longitudinal comparison, and measurement). The main score is the actionable-error count, both interpretable and auditable. Candidate findings are graded correct, partial, or incorrect, and unmatched findings are counted as missed or hallucinated. Triage and actionable safety recall/precision and per-subset views add complementary, deployment-oriented lenses. Across two expert benchmarks, RadMatch is the most clinically aligned metric, matching inter-radiologist agreement on ReXVal and more than doubling the best prior metric on the harder RadEvalExpert. Relying only on few-shot prompting, it is designed to extend to other modalities and anatomies. We will release RadMatch as open-source code with an interactive dashboard for inspecting results.

</details>


### [105] [CameraEditor: Camera-Controlled Image Editing via Video-Prior Sequential Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01479)
*Xin Shen,Chengyou Jia,Keshuo Xing,Zifeng Zhu,Changliang Xia,Bowen Ping,Zhuohang Dang,Hangwei Qian,Minnan Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CameraEditor框架，将相机参数控制的图像编辑问题从空间域重构为时间序列预测任务，利用视频扩散模型的时间一致性，结合显式几何感知模块和动态参考路由机制，并通过动态全景裁剪和中间过渡帧插入实现大视角变换下的内容保真与几何精确编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像编辑模型擅长语义和风格操作，但在显式相机参数控制方面表现不佳。当面临大幅度视角变化时，指令驱动模型要么产生结构性撕裂，要么生成忽略几何指令的保守输出。文本指令存在固有的模糊性，无法精确描述具体相机几何变换。

Method: CameraEditor将相机控制编辑定义为时间序列预测任务，具体包括：(1) 显式几何感知模块，用于理解相机位姿变换；(2) 动态参考路由机制，通过动态全景裁剪构建几何严谨的视觉参考对，规避文本指令歧义；(3) 插入中间过渡帧以分解大幅视角变化，提供时间缓冲以保持内容身份和空间一致性；(4) 利用视频扩散模型的时间一致性来保证编辑帧序列的连贯性。

Result: 构建了含5,760个实例的训练数据集，并独立贡献了CamEditor-Bench测试套件（462个测试案例，模型无关）。大量实验表明CameraEditor在相机控制精度和源图像身份保持方面达到最先进水平，优于现有方法。

Conclusion: CameraEditor验证了将空间视角编辑问题转化为时间序列预测任务的有效性，通过视频扩散模型的时间一致性成功解决了大视角变换下图像编辑的结构完整性与几何精确性问题，并为该领域提供了标准化的评估基准。

Abstract: Beyond semantic content, camera parameters play a pivotal role in dictating the geometric perspective and appearance of any given image. While recent image editing models excel at semantic and stylistic manipulation, they struggle with explicit camera parameter control. When handling large perspective shifts, instruction-driven models face a dilemma: they either suffer from structural tearing or generate conservative outputs that ignore geometric instructions. To address this, we introduce CameraEditor, a framework that reformulates camera-controlled editing from a spatial problem into a temporal sequence prediction task. By leveraging the temporal coherence of video diffusion models, our approach integrates an explicit geometric perception module with a dynamic reference routing mechanism. This allows us to construct geometrically rigorous visual reference pairs via dynamic panorama cropping, overcoming the ambiguity of text-based instructions. Furthermore, CameraEditor strategically inserts intermediate transition frames to decompose large perspective shifts, providing a robust temporal buffer that preserves content identity and spatial coherence. We construct a training dataset of 5,760 instances. As an independent contribution, we introduce CamEditor-Bench, a model-agnostic evaluation suite of 462 test cases. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CameraEditor achieves state-of-the-art camera control precision and source identity preservation, outperforming existing methods.

</details>


### [106] [Benchmarking Spatial, Spectral, and Self-Supervised Cues for Face Forgery Detection under Realistic Degradation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01511)
*Lucas Cunha,Lucas Sotomaior,Lucas Gasperin,Beatriz Caldas,Eduardo Pianovski,Rayson Laroca*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于MFFI数据集的人脸伪造检测标准化基准，系统评估了六类模型族（CNN、Transformer、冻结DINOv3）在干净与退化测试集上的表现。结果表明干净集性能不能可靠预测鲁棒性：Xception在干净集上最优（0.884 AUC）但在退化集上严重下降，而冻结DINOv3仅用线性分类头即在退化集上取得最佳结果（0.726 AUC）。傅里叶域特征需与RGB结合才有用，纯频谱特征逊于空间特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人脸伪造检测器在受控基准上表现良好，但在真实场景的图像退化（压缩、缩放、模糊）下可靠性有限。缺乏标准化的退化评估协议导致无法准确衡量检测器的真实鲁棒性。

Method: 使用MFFI数据集构建包含干净与退化两种测试分区的基准，系统比较六种模型族（卷积网络、Transformer、冻结自监督DINOv3骨干），并评估空间、频谱和混合输入表示的性能。同时通过定性归因图分析不同检测器的关注区域。

Result: Xception+RGB在干净集最优（0.884 ROC-AUC）但在困难退化分区显著下降；冻结DINOv3在退化集最强（0.726 ROC-AUC），且仅需训练线性分类头。傅里叶提示与RGB结合时最有效，纯频谱输入始终不及空间表示。归因图显示CNN关注局部伪影，而DINOv3依赖更广泛的整体面部结构。

Conclusion: 研究强调需建立退化评估协议来衡量检测器的真实鲁棒性，并指出自监督视觉表示（如DINOv3）是面向鲁棒人脸伪造检测的有前景方向。代码已开源。

Abstract: Face forgery detectors often achieve strong results on controlled benchmarks, but their reliability under realistic image degradations remains limited. This paper presents a standardized benchmark for face forgery detection using the Multi-Dimensional Face Forgery Image (MFFI) dataset and evaluates performance on both clean and degraded test partitions. We compare six model families, including convolutional networks, transformer-based models, and a frozen self-supervised DINOv3 backbone, across spatial, spectral, and hybrid input representations. The results show that clean-set performance is not a reliable indicator of robustness under compression, resizing, and blurring. Xception with RGB obtains the best clean performance, reaching 0.884 mean ROC-AUC, but degrades substantially on the harder partition. In contrast, frozen DINOv3 achieves the strongest degraded-set result, with 0.726 mean ROC-AUC, while training only a linear classification head. The representation analysis indicates that Fourier-domain cues are most useful when combined with RGB information, whereas purely spectral inputs consistently underperform spatial representations. Qualitative attribution maps further suggest that convolutional detectors focus on localized artifacts, while DINOv3 relies on broader facial structure. These findings reinforce the need for degraded evaluation protocols and highlight self-supervised visual representations as a promising direction for robust face forgery detection. Our source code is publicly available at https://github.com/lucasdocunha/FaceForgery-Benchmark/.

</details>


### [107] [TempCloze: Can Video-LLMs Identify the Missing Middle?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01515)
*Wenqi Pei,Henry Hengyuan Zhao,Yilai Liu,Jiahao Meng,Han Chen,Ziyu Wang,Hongyang Du*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了TempCloze，一个视频完形填空基准，用于评估Video-LLMs的视觉时序推理能力。通过提供视频的首尾片段并要求模型从四个候选中识别真实缺失的中间片段，该基准消除了语言中介的捷径。评估10个专有和21个开源模型发现，时序对齐（Alignment）是主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频时序推理基准常被语言中介，模型可以利用选项措辞、答案相关性或语言先验等捷径，而非真正的视觉时序推理。需要设计一个减少语言捷径的基准以可靠评估模型的视觉时序理解能力。

Method: 构建TempCloze基准：(1) 提供给定视频的开始和结束片段，模型从四个候选中识别真实缺失的中间片段；(2) 包含1,521个精心筛选的视频，主要来自长镜头和第一人称视角视频；(3) 设计同源干扰项，沿三个维度构建：Semantic（什么事件应发生）、Alignment（何时发生）、Progression（如何展开）；(4) 通过共享场景和物体减少外观线索。进一步基于TempCloze-Mixed和TempCloze-Hard进行错误模式和行为敏感性分析。

Result: 对10个专有和21个开源Video-LLMs的评估表明，Alignment是主要瓶颈——模型通常能识别合理的语义内容和局部事件推进，但在时序对齐方面存在困难。错误模式与行为敏感性分析考察了错误来源及候选顺序、上下文方向、可见跨度、帧密度和测试时缩放等对模型选择的影响。

Conclusion: TempCloze揭示了当前Video-LLMs在视觉时序推理中时序对齐能力的显著不足，为社区提供了更纯粹的时序推理测试平台，并指出了未来改进方向。

Abstract: Temporal reasoning benchmarks for Video-LLMs are often mediated by language, leaving room for linguistic shortcuts from option wording, answer correlations, or language priors. To reduce such shortcuts, we introduce TempCloze, a video cloze benchmark for evaluating visual temporal reasoning in Video-LLMs. Given the beginning and ending clips of a video, models must identify the true missing middle from four candidates. TempCloze contains 1,521 carefully filtered videos from seven sources, mainly long-take and egocentric videos. We construct same-source distractors along three dimensions: Semantic asks what event should happen, Alignment probes when it should occur, and Progression tests how it should unfold, while shared scenes and objects reduce appearance cues. Our evaluation of 10 proprietary and 21 open-source Video-LLMs reveals Alignment as the primary bottleneck: models often recognize plausible semantic content and local event progression but struggle with temporal alignment. We further conduct error pattern and behavioral sensitivity analyses on TempCloze-Mixed and TempCloze-Hard with four representative models to examine where errors arise and how candidate order, context direction, visible span, frame density, and test-time scaling influence model choices.

</details>


### [108] [DualDiff3D: Dual Structure-Appearance Diffusion Priors for Reliability-Enhanced 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01516)
*Qian Wang,Yu Wang,Weiqi Li,Xinhua Cheng,Xiandong Meng,Ronggang Wang,Jian Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DualDiff，一种利用双扩散先验结合结构-外观注意力(SAA)模块来细化从低质量3D重建中渲染的退化新视图的方法，并在此基础上构建DualDiff3D三维重建框架，通过可靠增强的Render-Refine-Optimize循环逐步优化3DGS模型，在有限视角重建场景下显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D高斯泼溅在输入视角有限时重建质量差，新视图渲染存在伪影。现有方法用扩散先验时通常将渲染视图和参考视图在单一网络中拼接处理，忽视了不同视角间应保持外观相似但结构随视角移动而变化的内在特性，导致两类属性冲突进而产生模糊。

Method: 提出DualDiff管线：保留一个扩散分支提取低质量渲染视图的结构信息，引入另一分支确保与参考视图的外观一致性，并通过结构-外观注意力(SAA)模块融合两条分支的指导信息。进一步提出DualDiff3D框架，集成可靠性增强的Render-Refine-Optimize(RRO)循环，逐步稳健地将细化后的新视图融入3DGS重建过程中。

Result: 大量实验表明该方法在仅推理(inference-only)设置下即超越现有最先进方法，通过训练可进一步提升性能。

Conclusion: 通过解耦结构信息提取与外观一致性保持这两个任务，并配合可靠的迭代优化循环，可以有效缓解有限视角下3DGS重建的退化问题，实现更高质量的三维重建与新视图合成。

Abstract: While 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has revolutionized 3D reconstruction and novel-view synthesis, scenarios with limited input views often lead to poor reconstruction quality and artifacts in rendered novel views. Recent efforts attempt to utilize powerful diffusion priors, yet they typically process rendered and reference views concatenated along an additional dimension in a single network. These methods overlook an inherent nature that different views should maintain appearance similarity but differ in structure due to view shifts, leading to blur caused by conflicts between the two properties. In this paper, we propose DualDiff, a novel pipeline that leverages dual diffusion priors with a Structure-Appearance Attention (SAA) module to introduce reference guidance for refining low-quality novel views rendered from flawed 3D representations. Specifically, we retain one diffusion branch to focus on extracting structural information from the low-quality novel views, while introducing another branch to ensure appearance consistency with reference views. Furthermore, we present a 3D reconstruction framework named DualDiff3D, which integrates a reliability-enhanced Render-Refine-Optimize (RRO) loop to progressively and robustly incorporate the refined novel views, yielding more accurate 3DGS. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach outperforms state-of-the-art methods even in the inference-only setting, with further performance gains achievable through training. Our code and pre-trained weights are available at https://github.com/Akaneqwq/DualDiff3D.

</details>


### [109] [Revisiting Cross-View Completion: Self-Supervised Pre-Training via Reconstruction Error Comparison](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01530)
*Thibaut Loiseau,Guillaume Bourmaud,Vincent Lepetit*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Gekko是一种新的自监督3D视觉预训练方法，通过联合进行跨视图补全和掩码自编码，并预测两者之间的相对误差改进作为共视区域的代理信号，从而在无3D标注的情况下为所有掩码区域提供双目信号。在零样本对应关系估计、相对位姿估计和点图回归任务上一致优于CroCo，且可从原始视频直接训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于跨视图补全的自监督预训练（如CroCo）虽然能从图像对的共视区域学习强3D特征，但在非共视区域，参考视图几乎不提供重建信息，导致这些区域隐式地退化为单目训练信号，限制了模型学习真正的双目/多视角几何能力。作者希望将这一局限转化为有用的信号。

Method: Gekko在同一网络中联合执行三个任务：跨视图补全、掩码自编码，以及逐像素预测跨视图重建误差相对于掩码自编码误差的改善程度。这个相对改善量可作为共视性的自监督代理指标（改善大=共视区，改善小=非共视区），从而为所有掩码区域提供额外的双目训练信号。此外还提出基于步长（stride）的课程学习策略，使模型可直接从原始视频训练，无需3D预处理。

Result: 在相同架构和训练数据下，Gekko在零样本对应关系估计、相对位姿估计和点图回归上一致优于CroCo：在最严格相对位姿阈值下准确率提升达6倍，ETH3D上端点误差降低22%。学到的额外通道本身是未见场景上的强共视检测器，冻结特征优于同规模或更大规模的已发布跨视图骨干网络。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模跨视图补全中非共视区域的局限性并转化为辅助监督信号，Gekko在不依赖任何真值3D标注的情况下提升了3D视觉下游任务的性能，并支持从原始视频端到端训练，消除了先前方法所需的繁琐3D预处理步骤，为自监督3D视觉预训练提供了更高效且更强的范式。

Abstract: Self-supervised pre-training via cross-view completion learns strong features for 3D vision from co-visible regions of image pairs. However, the reference view provides little information for reconstructing non-co-visible patches, implicitly yielding a monocular training signal in these regions. We introduce Gekko, which turns this limitation into a useful signal. The relative improvement of the cross-view reconstruction error over a masked-autoencoder error is a self-supervised proxy for co-visibility: large improvements indicate co-visible regions, negligible ones non-co-visible areas. Gekko is a network, trained from scratch, that jointly performs cross-view completion, masked autoencoding, and per-pixel prediction of this relative improvement, providing an additional binocular signal for all masked regions without any ground-truth 3D annotation. Under identical architectures and training data, Gekko consistently outperforms CroCo on zero-shot correspondence estimation, relative pose estimation, and pointmap regression, with up to 6 times higher accuracy at the strictest relative-pose threshold and a 22% drop in end-point error on ETH3D. The extra channel it learns is itself a strong co-visibility detector on unseen scenes, and Gekko's frozen features outperform released cross-view backbones of comparable or larger size. It can also be trained directly from raw videos with a simple stride-based curriculum, removing the cumbersome 3D preprocessing prior methods require while matching models trained on curated data. Code and pre-trained models are publicly available.

</details>


### [110] [What, Where, and How: Probing Spatiotemporal Representations in Video Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01551)
*Sharon S. Musa,Fereshteh Forghani,Harrish Thasarathan,Sonia Joseph,Matthew Kowal,Konstantinos G. Derpanis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对自监督视频基础模型（V-JEPA 2 和 VideoMAE-v2）进行系统的逐层分析，探究其编码的视觉概念、概念出现的层位置及几何组织方式。研究发现模型编码相机运动（最高>90% ROC AUC，出现在网络深度60-70%处）和中等程度异常检测（>60% ROC AUC），但在直觉物理任务上接近随机水平。特征在表示空间中形成平滑的低维轨迹，基于此提出几何感知样条引导（steering）方法用于插值相机运动，生成比线性插值更平滑的视频。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督视频基础模型虽已学习到丰富的时空表征，但这些表征具体编码了哪些视觉概念、这些概念在Transformer层间何处涌现、以及它们在几何空间中如何组织，这些问题仍然模糊不清。作者希望通过系统性逐层分析来揭示黑箱模型的内部表征机制。

Method: 对V-JEPA 2和VideoMAE-v2两个模型进行逐层分析，使用轻量级探针（lightweight probes）训练来发现三类时间接地（temporally grounded）属性：相机运动理解、直觉物理和异常检测。在分类任务之外，还分析单个视频的时间特征在表征空间中是否形成平滑的低维轨迹，并基于几何特性应用样条引导（spline-based steering）进行相机运动的潜在表征插值。

Result: 两类模型均能编码相机运动，最佳性能（>90% ROC AUC）出现在网络深度60-70%处；异常检测达到中等水平（>60% ROC AUC）；但在直觉物理任务上接近随机，表明对深层物理推理的编码有限。视频时间特征在表示空间中形成平滑低维轨迹，相机运动不仅可线性解码还具有几何组织性。样条引导插值比线性插值产生更平滑的轨迹和更连贯的时间进展。

Conclusion: 自监督视频模型在浅至中层更擅长编码低层运动属性（如相机运动），但对直觉物理等深层物理推理的表征能力有限。中层特征（60-70%深度）是运动信息的集中涌现区域。表示空间中的轨迹几何结构可利用几何感知方法（样条引导）进行编辑和操作，实现更自然的视频内容插值。

Abstract: Self-supervised video foundation models learn rich spatiotemporal representations, yet it remains unclear what visual concepts these representations encode, where they emerge across transformer layers, and how they are geometrically organized. In this work, we tackle these three questions through a systematic layer-wise analysis of V-JEPA 2 and VideoMAE-v2. We leverage lightweight probes trained to discover three temporally grounded properties: (i) camera motion understanding, (ii) intuitive physics, and (iii) anomaly detection. Both models encode camera motion, with best results ($>90$ ROC AUC) emerging at 60-70% of network depth, and achieve moderate anomaly detection performance ($>60$ ROC AUC), but remain near chance on intuitive-physics tasks, suggesting a limited encoding of deeper physical reasoning. Beyond classification, we find that temporal features from individual videos form smooth low-dimensional trajectories in representation space, suggesting that camera motion is not only linearly decodable but also geometrically organized. Based on these results, we apply geometry-aware spline-based steering in the model's latent representations to interpolate camera motion, yielding steered videos with smoother trajectories and more coherent temporal progression than linear interpolation.

</details>


### [111] [BS: Take the Hint - Interactive Multitracer PET/CT Lesion Segmentation with a Scribble-Conditioned ResEnc U-Net](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01554)
*Marven Sherif,Amgad Elmasry,Youssef Ghazal,Ayman Elghotni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于涂鸦（scribble）条件化的残差编码器U-Net用于全身PET/CT交互式病灶分割，模型输入包含CT、PET及前景/背景涂鸦四通道，利用autoPET-III预训练权重初始化并逐fold微调，通过主动脉血池参考归一化PET强度，推理时对5个fold模型进行softmax集成。实验表明交互式校正能将Dice从0.554提升至0.751。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全身PET/CT中的自动化病灶分割面临生理示踪剂摄取模式多样以及不同示踪剂间病灶表现差异巨大的挑战。autoPET/CT V挑战赛通过引入用户涂鸦交互信息使分割变得可交互，本工作旨在利用交互信号提升分割性能，并研究交互对模型间差异的补偿作用。

Method: 提出一种涂鸦条件化的残差编码器U-Net，输入为四通道：CT、PET及前景/背景稀疏涂鸦图。从autoPET-III冠军权重初始化并扩展为四通道，涂鸦通道零初始化以保持预训练表示不变。每个fold从对应autoPET-III checkpoint微调，确保验证集不参与预训练。PET强度基于CT分割得到的主动脉血池参考进行每扫描归一化。推理时对5个fold模型的softmax输出在滑动窗口patch上取平均，再进行高斯加权拼接。

Result: 在挑战赛五折划分上，无涂鸦时平均Dice为0.554、病灶级F1为0.528；经过五轮交互修正后分别提升至0.751和0.733。约85%的增益来自第一轮涂鸦，且各fold模型间的性能差异在交互过程中缩小了五倍，说明交互大幅补偿了模型无辅助时的分割能力差异。

Conclusion: 交互式涂鸦条件化有效提升了全身PET/CT病灶分割精度，预训练初始化策略与主动脉血池归一化设计合理，交互的引入不仅带来显著的性能提升，还显著降低了不同fold模型间的性能差距，表明交互可以在很大程度上弥补模型本身的分割能力不足。

Abstract: Automated lesion segmentation in whole-body PET/CT is complicated by the variety of physiological tracer uptake patterns and by the differing appearance of lesions across tracers. The autoPET/CT V challenge addresses this by making segmentation interactive: user scribbles marking foreground and background are supplied alongside the image, and the algorithm is expected to exploit them. We present our submission, a scribble-conditioned residual encoder U-Net operating on four input channels: CT, PET, and a sparse scribble map for each of foreground and background. The network is initialised from the autoPET-III winning weights and extended from two to four input channels, with the two scribble channels zero-initialised so that the pretrained representation is preserved exactly at initialisation. Every model is fine-tuned per fold from the corresponding autoPET-III fold checkpoint, so that no validation case is seen during pretraining. PET intensities are normalised against a per-scan aorta blood-pool reference derived from a CT segmentation, which removes tracer- and centre-specific scaling without requiring lesion labels. At inference the five fold models are ensembled by averaging their softmax outputs per sliding-window patch, before Gaussian-weighted stitching. On the challenge's five-fold split, with each fold evaluated on its own validation cases, mean Dice is 0.554 and mean lesion-level F1 is 0.528 without scribbles, rising to 0.751 and 0.733 after five correction rounds. About 85% of that gain follows the first scribble, and the spread between fold models narrows five-fold over the same rounds, so interaction largely compensates for how well or badly a given model segments unaided.

</details>


### [112] [H3-World: Turning Language Understanding into World Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01560)
*Danze Chen,Zeqing Wang,Ziyue Lin,Xingyi Yang,Yeying Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: H3-World是一个高效框架，将33B参数的MiniMax-H3视频生成器转变为交互式世界模型。其核心发现是大型视频生成器具备通过自然语言进行零样本控制角色行为和摄像机运动的能力。H3-World通过将动作表示为角色和摄像机指令的结构化组合并引入时间注意力路由来实现精确的时间级控制，仅需8000个游戏样本、10000步LoRA优化和0.199%的可训练参数即可实现有效控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大型视频生成器能力增强，语言正涌现为自然的控制接口。然而现有的语言控制是粗粒度的，缺乏时间精度，无法实现精细的交互式世界控制。作者旨在将这种粗粒度语言接口转化为精确、时间上锚定的世界控制，而无需引入专门的动作模块。

Method: 1) 将每个动作表示为角色指令与摄像机指令的结构化组合，并与对应的时序视频潜在特征对齐；2) 提出时间注意力路由（temporal attention routing），将每条指令限制在其对应的时间区间内，减少跨动作的控制泄漏；3) 直接复用大规模视频预训练中学到的语义表示，仅需轻量适配（LoRA微调）。

Result: 在仅8000个游戏样本、10000步LoRA优化步骤和0.199%可训练参数的情况下，H3-World实现了有效的角色控制和摄像机控制，同时保持了良好的生成质量，并能泛化到未见过的场景。

Conclusion: 该结果表明，大型视频生成器中涌现的控制能力可以被高效地转化为交互式世界控制，验证了大规模视频预训练语义表示的可复用性，为构建可交互世界模型提供了一条低成本、高效的新路径。

Abstract: We present H3-World, an efficient framework that turns the 33B MiniMax-H3 video generator into an interactive world model. Our key finding is that, as large video generators become more capable, language is emerging as a natural interface for control. MiniMax-H3, for example, already supports zero-shot control of character behavior and camera motion through natural-language instructions. Building on this, H3-World turns this coarse language interface into precise, temporally grounded world control, without introducing dedicated action modules. Specifically, we represent each action as a structured combination of character and camera instructions, and align them with the corresponding temporal video latents. To make the control temporally precise, we further introduce temporal attention routing, which restricts each instruction to its intended time interval and reduces control leakage across actions. Importantly, H3-World directly reuses the semantic representations learned during large-scale video pretraining and requires only lightweight adaptation. With only 8,000 gameplay samples, 10,000 LoRA optimization steps, and 0.199% trainable parameters, H3-World achieves effective character and camera control while preserving strong generation quality. It also generalizes to unseen scenarios. These results show that the control capabilities emerging in large video generators can be efficiently transformed into interactive world control.

</details>


### [113] [SpatialGuard: Harness-Guided Verifiable Spatial Reasoning for Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01582)
*Ziyun Qian,Zizhi Chen,Yizhou Liu,Mingyang Sun,Dingkang Yang,Lihua Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SpatialGuard，一个面向复杂3D空间文本到图像生成的布局引导框架。它将提示解析为可编辑的3D布局，通过视觉实现器生成条件图像，并用视觉对齐评判器验证提示、布局和图像间的一致性，引入布局管理器在迭代中保持约束稳定，将复杂空间生成从隐式提示跟随转变为可验证的规划-实现-验证-修复过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提示驱动或布局条件方法虽然提升了可控性，但在视觉采样之前缺乏可优化且可验证的空间中介；导致多轮生成中对象关系、遮挡、可见性和相机约束逐渐退化。

Method: SpatialGuard包含三个核心组件：(1) 空间布局架构师(Spatial Layout Architect)将提示解析为面向图像合成的3D布局；(2) 视觉实现器(Visual Realizer)将布局实现为视觉条件和候选图像；(3) 视觉对齐评判器(Visual Alignment Critic)验证提示、布局和图像间的一致性。此外，布局管理器(Layout Harness)围绕可编辑布局状态组织规则约束、工具调用、共享知识和反馈循环，确保迭代期间约束的稳定性。

Result: 全面实验表明，SpatialGuard在复杂3D空间布局生成上达到最先进性能，在空间保真度方面优于现有文本到图像和布局控制基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过引入可编辑且可验证的3D布局作为空间中介，并配合验证-修复的反馈循环机制，SpatialGuard将复杂3D空间生成转化为可规划、可实现、可验证的流程，有效解决了对象关系、遮挡和相机约束在多轮生成中的退化问题。

Abstract: Complex 3D spatial text to image generation requires models to convert natural language into stable visual geometry, not merely semantic appearance. Existing prompt-driven or layout-conditioned methods improve controllability, but often lack an optimizable and verifiable spatial intermediary before visual sampling. As a result, object relations, occlusion, visibility, and camera constraints can decay during multi-round generation. This paper presents SpatialGuard, a structured layout-guided framework for complex 3D spatial text-to-image generation. SpatialGuard parses prompts into image synthesis-oriented 3D layouts through a Spatial Layout Architect, realizes them as visual conditions and candidate images through a Visual Realizer, and uses a Visual Alignment Critic to validate consistency among prompt, layout, and image. To keep constraints stable across iterations, SpatialGuard introduces a Layout Harness that organizes rule constraints, tool invocation, shared knowledge, and feedback loops around the editable layout state. This design turns complex spatial generation from implicit prompt following into a verifiable process of planning, realization, validation, and repair. Comprehensive experiments show that SpatialGuard achieves state-of-the-art performance in complex 3D spatial layout generation and improves spatial faithfulness over existing text-to-image and layout control baselines.

</details>


### [114] [A Benchmark for Vehicle Attribute Classification in Cross-Domain Surveillance Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01584)
*Sergio M. Silva,Otavio T. Remer,Gabriel E. Lima,Lucas Wojcik,Rayson Laroca,David Menotti*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出UVIB基准，用于评估无约束场景下车辆属性分析（朝向、VMMR适用性、颜色清晰度）的三项任务，包含84,835张来自七个巴西公开数据集的车辆图像，并在混合域、跨域和跨数据集协议下评估四种代表性架构，发现域偏移对性能的影响大于架构选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有车辆属性分析模型在受控条件下训练后，在真实监控场景中因视角、遮挡、光照和传感器特性变化而性能下降，缺乏能够显式评估超出标准域内准确率的操作性鲁棒性的基准和评估协议。

Method: 构建UVIB基准数据集，整合七个巴西公有数据集的84,835张车辆图像，划分为监控域和一般采集域两个域，提供统一的二分类标注（前/后朝向、VMMR适用性、颜色清晰度），并采用混合域、跨域和跨数据集三种评估协议对EfficientNetV2-S、ResNet-50、ViT/B-16和YOLO11s-cls四种架构进行评测。

Result: 实验表明域偏移对模型性能的影响显著大于架构选择，跨域设置下性能大幅下降，尤其在VMMR适用性和颜色清晰度任务上；朝向任务泛化更可靠，VMMR适用性受类别不平衡和模糊遮挡影响，颜色清晰度对照明和传感器模态高度敏感。

Conclusion: 现有模型在操作鲁棒性方面存在明显不足，需要能显式度量超越标准域内精度的基准与评估协议；UVIB基准已公开，可作为后续研究与评估的参考平台。

Abstract: Vehicle attribute analysis is a key component of Intelligent Transportation Systems (ITS), supporting applications such as vehicle identification, traffic monitoring, and forensic investigation. However, models trained under controlled conditions often degrade in real surveillance scenarios due to changes in viewpoint, occlusion, illumination, and sensor characteristics. This paper introduces Unconstrained Vehicle Identification Benchmark (UVIB), a benchmark for evaluating three operational vehicle-analysis tasks: front/rear orientation, occlusion-related suitability for Vehicle Make and Model Recognition (VMMR), and color clarity. The benchmark contains 84,835 vehicle images from seven public Brazilian datasets, grouped into surveillance and general acquisition domains, with unified binary annotations that were not jointly available in the original sources. Four representative architectures, EfficientNetV2-S, ResNet-50, ViT/B-16, and YOLO11s-cls, are evaluated under mixed-domain, cross-domain, and cross-dataset protocols. The results show that domain shift has a stronger impact than architecture choice, with substantial degradation in cross-domain settings, especially for VMMR suitability and color clarity. While orientation generalizes more reliably, VMMR suitability remains affected by class imbalance and ambiguous occlusions, and color clarity is highly sensitive to illumination and sensor modality. These findings highlight the need for benchmarks and evaluation protocols that explicitly measure operational robustness beyond standard in-domain accuracy. The proposed benchmark is publicly available at https://github.com/UFPR-IPASP-PR/uvib-vehicle-attributes/.

</details>


### [115] [UI-VISA: U-Net Initialized Vascular Image Segmentation Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01598)
*Asees Kaur,Suzanne S. Sindi,Erica M. Rutter*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出UI-VISA，一种结合U-Net与CNN引导的区域生长算法的混合血管分割流水线，用于DSA图像中细小、细长且分支的血管结构分割。该方法利用U-Net前景预测作为种子点初始化区域生长，迭代细化分割以增强结构连通性。在26张DSA图像上的5折交叉验证中，UI-VISA在Dice和clDice指标上均优于单独的U-Net和先前基于区域生长的方法VISA，其中clDice的改善具有统计学显著性（p=0.023）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DSA图像中的血管具有细、长、分支多的特点。纯像素级深度学习方法（如U-Net）虽具有强泛化分割性能，但缺乏显式的结构连通性约束，常在细小血管区域产生碎片化或不连续的分割结果；而区域生长算法虽能保持空间上下文和拓扑连续性，但对种子点初始化高度敏感且计算开销大。作者旨在融合两者互补优势，以同时获得深度学习的特征表达能力和区域生长的拓扑约束能力。

Method: UI-VISA采用混合流水线架构：首先使用U-Net生成前景预测，将预测结果作为种子点输入到CNN引导的区域生长算法中；随后区域生长算法通过迭代过程施加局部连通性约束，逐步细化分割结果，恢复U-Net容易遗漏或过度预测的精细血管细节。该方法将U-Net的全局特征学习能力与区域生长的局部拓扑保持能力有机结合。

Result: 在26张DSA图像上进行5折交叉验证，UI-VISA相比独立U-Net和先前基于区域生长的方法VISA，在平均Dice和clDice得分上均达到最高。配对Wilcoxon符号秩检验显示，clDice的改进具有统计学显著性（p=0.023），与该方法旨在保持血管连通性的设计目标一致；而Dice的改进未达到显著性水平（p=0.104）。

Conclusion: UI-VISA通过将U-Net的预测作为种子点引导CNN驱动的区域生长，有效解决了像素级深度学习方法在血管分割中缺乏结构连通性的问题，显著提升了血管连通性指标（clDice），验证了混合架构在细长血管结构分割任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Accurate segmentation of vascular structures in digital subtraction angiography (DSA) images remains challenging due to the thin, elongated, and branching nature of blood vessels. Pixel-wise deep learning approaches such as U-Net achieve strong general-purpose segmentation performance but often produce fragmented or discontinuous predictions in fine vascular regions, since they do not explicitly enforce structural connectivity. Region growing algorithms preserve spatial context and topological continuity, but are highly sensitive to seed point initialization and can be computationally expensive. We propose UI-VISA (U-Net Initialized Vascular Image Segmentation Architecture), a hybrid pipeline that combines the complementary strengths of both approaches. UI-VISA uses U-Net's foreground predictions as informed seed points for a CNN-guided region growing algorithm, which then iteratively refines the segmentation by enforcing local connectivity and recovering fine vessel details that U-Net alone tends to miss or over-predict. We evaluate UI-VISA against standalone U-Net and a prior region-growing-based method (VISA) using 5-fold cross-validation on 26 DSA images. UI-VISA achieves the highest mean Dice and clDice scores across folds, and a paired Wilcoxon signed-rank test shows the improvement in clDice is statistically significant ($p=0.023$), consistent with the method's design goal of preserving vascular connectivity, while the improvement in Dice does not reach significance ($p=0.104$).

</details>


### [116] [Uncovering Understanding-Generation Synergy in Native Unified Multimodal Models: From Representation, Task to System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01607)
*Penghao Wu,Haiwen Diao,Weichen Fan,Lewei Lu,Dahua Lin,Ziwei Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文在无预训练视觉先验的受控原生结构中，从表征、任务和系统三个层面系统研究了统一多模态模型（UMM）中视觉理解与生成目标的关系，发现二者在表征和任务层面存在正向协同，但共享同一计算路径时会产生不对称的支配效应；通过任务解耦架构、共享知识利用和端到端优化可将共存转化为真正的协同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一多模态模型（UMM）将视觉理解和生成功能集成于单一模型，但功能层面的统一并不能保证学习层面的协同——两个目标可能是相互强化、竞争容量或仅共存。现有研究缺乏对这种关系在受控条件下的系统性理解，尤其是在无预训练视觉先验的原生设定下，无法厘清理解与生成目标之间的真实交互机制。

Method: 研究在三个层面展开系统分析：（1）表征层面：测试各目标对另一目标提供的信号贡献，并对比共享计算路径与任务解耦架构（分割冲突的视觉计算但保留语义交互）的效果；（2）任务层面：通过三个案例研究考察理解与生成任务依赖共享知识时的双向迁移效果；（3）系统层面：对比端到端UMM与匹配的规划器-执行器流水线在需要同时进行图像理解和生成的复杂任务上的表现。

Result: 表征层面：生成丰富了理解所需的视觉特征，理解增强了用于生成的视觉-语言对齐；但两者共享同一计算路径时会发生一方支配另一方的不对称退化，任务解耦架构可避免这种退化。任务层面：理解与生成任务依赖共享知识时呈现正向双向迁移。系统层面：端到端UMM在复杂任务上优于规划器-执行器流水线。

Conclusion: UMM的价值超越了统一接口本身：适当的专业化分工（任务解耦）、共享任务知识的利用以及端到端优化，能够将理解与生成目标从简单的共存转变为真正的协同增益。

Abstract: While unified multimodal models (UMMs) jointly perform visual understanding and generation within a single model, functional unification does not guarantee learning synergy: the two objectives may reinforce each other, compete for capacity, or merely coexist. We investigate their relationship at the representation, task, and system levels in a controlled, structurally native setting without pretrained vision priors. At the representation level, we find that each objective provides useful signal to the other: generation enriches the visual features learned for understanding, while understanding strengthens vision--language alignment for generation. However, when both objectives are forced through the same computation path, one tends to dominate. A task-decoupled architecture that specializes conflicting visual computation while preserving semantic interaction avoids this asymmetric degradation. At the task level, through three case studies, we find positive bidirectional transfer when understanding and generation tasks rely on shared knowledge. At the system level, we show that an end-to-end UMM outperforms a matched planner--executor pipeline on complex tasks that explicitly require both image understanding and generation. Together, these results show that the value of UMMs extends beyond a unified interface: appropriate specialization, shared task knowledge, and end-to-end optimization can turn coexistence into synergy.

</details>


<div id='cs.MM'></div>

# cs.MM [[Back]](#toc)

### [117] [Can LLMs Design Video Coding Tools? A Case Study on Planar Mode](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01535)
*Yingwen Zhang,Meng Wang,Liqiang He,Shiqi Wang*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文探讨大语言模型（LLM）能否设计视频编码工具，以Planar模式（视频编码标准中长期存在的帧内预测工具）为案例进行实证研究。实验采用生成与评估循环框架，LLM生成新的Planar预测器，编码器试验评估其编码性能，LLM根据反馈迭代优化。在VVenC快速预设下，LLM生成的模式以0.4%复杂度开销实现0.18%码率节省；在ECM中，直接替换和新增预测模式两种集成策略在低分辨率受限场景下均能带来编码增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频编码工具的设计极具挑战性，因其复杂算法之间紧密耦合，工具修改牵一发而动全身。作者希望探索LLM是否能够超越传统人工设计范式，自主生成和改进视频编码工具，这是一个高难度的跨领域创新尝试。

Method: 采用生成与评估循环（generation-and-evaluation loop）：LLM生成新的Planar预测器实现，编码器（VVenC和ECM）通过试验评估其编码性能，随后LLM基于评估反馈再生成精细化实现。在VVenC中直接替换默认Planar模式；在ECM中探索两种集成策略——直接替换新引入的方向性Planar模式，或将LLM生成的预测器作为带新语法元素的附加预测模式引入。

Result: 在VVenC快速预设下，LLM生成的Planar模式优于传统模式，标准基准上实现0.18%码率节省，仅引入0.4%复杂度开销。在ECM上，两种集成策略在受限的低分辨率设置下均获得编码增益。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了初步证据和实践洞见，表明LLM具备设计视频编码工具的潜力，同时揭示了其中仍存在的开放性挑战，为未来LLM驱动的编码工具设计方向奠定了基础。

Abstract: This paper explores whether large language models (LLMs) can design video coding tools, a highly challenging task due to the intricate algorithmic coupling of tool modifications. In particular, we present an empirical case study on the Planar mode, a long-standing intra prediction tool in video coding standards. Our experiments operate within a generation-and-evaluation loop, with the LLM generating new Planar predictors, encoder trials evaluating their coding performance, and the LLM re-generating refined implementations based on the evaluation feedback. We first examine directly replacing the default Planar mode in the Fraunhofer Versatile Video Encoder (VVenC) under its faster preset. Experimental results demonstrate that the LLM-generated mode can outperform the conventional Planar mode on this lightweight toolset, achieving 0.18% bitrate savings with 0.4% complexity overhead on the standard benchmark. We further extend our evaluation to the Enhanced Compression Model (ECM). Leveraging newly introduced directional Planar modes, we investigate two integration strategies: directly replacing them, and introducing the LLM-generated predictor as an additional prediction mode with new syntax elements. The empirical results suggest that both strategies can yield coding gains under a constrained low-resolution setting. Overall, this study offers preliminary evidence and practical insights, highlighting both the potential and open challenges of LLM-based coding tool design.

</details>
